Finally, our ablation studies confirm that persistent knowledge accumulation in the wiki is critical for effective skill evolution. These results demonstrate the benefits of systematically accumulating and refining agent experience for developing reusable and transferable skills.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.CP'></div>

# q-fin.CP [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [A Temporal Multiplex Graph Neural Network for Systemic Risk Transmission in Global Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27295)
*Nneka Umeorah,Tolulope Fadina*

Main category: q-fin.CP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的框架，利用时间异质多层图神经网络（Temporal Heterogeneous Multiplex Graph Neural Network）评估全球银行体系的系统性风险并识别传染渠道。通过构建包含银行基本面、CDS利差和宏观经济指标的季度面板数据，将其表示为动态多层网络（金融相似性、流动性共同运动、国家层面宏观经济关系），模型整合图卷积层、GRU循环动力学和可学习的融合门控，以捕捉不同传染渠道的时变依赖。实证表明该框架在短期CDS利差预测上优于传统计量、机器学习和基于图的基准方法，并提供可解释的系统性风险评估、国家间溢出效应分析和传染路径识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球银行体系系统性风险的评估和传染渠道的识别是一个关键但具有挑战性的问题。传统方法难以同时捕获银行间复杂交互、时间动态性和多种异质传染渠道（如金融相似性、流动性联动、宏观经济关联），需要更强大的建模框架来统一处理这些多维度的风险信号。

Method: 1) 构建季度面板数据（银行基本面+CDS利差+宏观指标）；2) 将其表示为动态多层网络，包含金融相似性层、流动性共同运动层和宏观关系层；3) 使用图卷积层捕获空间依赖，GRU循环网络捕获时间动态；4) 引入可学习的融合门控以自适应调节不同渠道的时变权重；5) 通过压力测试、国家溢出分析和边扰动分析提供解释性评估。

Result: 该框架在短期CDS利差变化预测中优于传统计量经济学、机器学习和基于图的基准方法；成功量化银行级系统性重要性、评估国家层面宏观冲击溢出效应，并通过边扰动分析揭示传染路径；鲁棒性测试确认预测精度和系统性风险排名的稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了时间异质多层图神经网络在全球系统性风险建模中的有效性和可解释性，不仅提升了预测性能，还为监管机构和政策制定者提供了识别传染渠道、评估系统性重要机构及量化跨境溢出效应的实用分析工具。

Abstract: This paper develops a unified framework for assessing systemic risk and identifying contagion channels in the global banking system using a Temporal Heterogeneous Multiplex Graph Neural Network. We construct a harmonised quarterly panel combining bank fundamentals, CDS spreads, and macroeconomic indicators, and represent these data as dynamic multiplex networks linking banks through financial similarity and liquidity co-movement, augmented with country-level macroeconomic relationships. The model integrates graph convolutional layers with recurrent GRU dynamics and incorporates a learnable fusion gate to capture time-varying reliance on alternative contagion channels. Empirical results show that the framework outperforms conventional econometric, machine learning, and graph-based benchmarks for short-term changes in CDS spreads. Beyond forecasting, we provide an interpretable framework to quantify bank-level systemic importance via stress testing, assess country-level spillovers under macroeconomic shocks, and uncover transmission pathways through edge perturbation analysis. Robustness tests confirm the stability of both predictive accuracy and systemic risk rankings.

</details>

### [216] [Graph-Based Modeling of Financial Volatility Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26127)
*Chuanzhen Wang,Alice Zhang,Wei Chen,Michael Brown*

Main category: q-fin.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出FA-GSTN（Finance-Aware Graph Spatio-Temporal Network），将隐含波动率（IV）曲面建模为时空图序列进行已实现波动率（RV）预测，通过图神经网络捕捉曲面时空动态，融入金融领域知识（期权Greeks）并设计多尺度时序平滑门控与自适应鲁棒损失函数，在大规模股票期权数据集上取得SOTA性能（R²达0.473），且在数据效率、市场压力期鲁棒性方面均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确预测已实现波动率对风险管理和衍生品定价至关重要。虽然隐含波动率曲面蕴含丰富信息，但现有方法将其视为静态图像，无法捕捉其内在的动态演化特性，导致预测性能受限。

Method: FA-GSTN将IV曲面构建为时空图序列：节点对应网格点，边编码自适应的日内空间依赖和显式的日间时间依赖；融合金融领域知识作为节点特征（如期权Greeks）；引入多尺度时间平滑门控和自适应鲁棒损失函数以应对高频噪声。

Result: 在大规模股票期权数据集上达到SOTA性能（R²最高0.473）；仅用一年数据训练时显著超越Vision Transformer基线（R² 0.372 vs 0.315）；在2020-2021市场压力期表现出更强的鲁棒性；消融研究验证了时空图结构、金融感知组件和噪声处理模块的重要作用。

Conclusion: 显式建模时间动态性和注入金融归纳偏置对准确且稳健的波动率预测具有显著价值，为金融时序预测提供了新的架构思路。

Abstract: Accurate forecasting of realized volatility ($RV$) is crucial for risk management and derivatives pricing. Although the implied volatility ($IV$) surface offers rich informational content, prevailing methods that treat it as a static image fail to capture its inherent dynamics. To overcome this limitation, we propose the Finance-Aware Graph Spatio-Temporal Network (FA-GSTN), a novel architecture that reframes $RV$ forecasting as modeling the evolution of a structured financial object. FA-GSTN builds a spatio-temporal graph sequence from the $IV$ surface, where nodes correspond to grid points and edges encode adaptive spatial (intra-day) and explicit temporal (inter-day) dependencies. The model incorporates domain knowledge through finance-aware node features (e.g., option Greeks) and tackles high-frequency noise via a multi-scale temporal smoothing gate coupled with an adaptive robust loss function. Comprehensive evaluations on a large-scale equity options dataset show that FA-GSTN sets a new state of the art, delivering superior predictive accuracy ($R^2$ up to 0.473). It also demonstrates remarkable data efficiency, substantially outperforming strong Vision Transformer baselines when trained on only one year of data ($R^2$: 0.372 vs. 0.315). Furthermore, the model exhibits enhanced robustness during periods of market stress, such as 2020--2021. Ablation studies confirm the vital roles of the spatio-temporal graph structure, finance-aware components, and integrated noise-handling modules. Our work underscores the substantial benefits of explicitly modeling temporal dynamics and infusing financial inductive biases for accurate and robust volatility forecasting.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [Multi-Objective Planning for Healthcare Facility Resilience: Mitigate Now or Respond Later?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26304)
*Gizem Toplu-Tutay,John J. Hasenbein,Matt Kammer-Kerwick,Erhan Kutanoglu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双目标两阶段随机优化模型，用于同时确定医疗设施的永久加固决策和情景依赖的疏散决策，以应对洪水风险。模型在最小化经济成本的同时，纳入考虑社会脆弱性的服务中断影响指标，并通过精确的Benders分解和可扩展的Lagrangian对偶方法求解。基于德克萨斯州3,752家医院和护理院的案例研究表明，单纯优化经济成本会系统性忽视社会脆弱区域的设施保护，而沿Pareto前沿适度移动可在有限额外成本下大幅减少中断影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洪水可能损害医疗设施、中断当地照护能力并引发高成本的病患疏散。应对这些风险需要在不确定性下进行长期韧性投资，但现有方法未能同时优化经济成本和社会公平性（即对脆弱人群的服务中断影响）。

Method: 构建双目标两阶段随机优化模型：第一阶段决定设施永久加固，第二阶段决定情景依赖的疏散。第一个目标最小化预期疏散、物理损坏和业务中断成本；第二个目标最小化结合服务丧失时长、规模及地方社会脆弱性权重的服务中断影响指数。求解方法包括精确的Benders分解和带定制原始恢复的可扩展Lagrangian对偶方法，两者嵌入自适应程序以构建信息丰富的Pareto前沿。

Result: 德克萨斯州3,752家医院和护理院的案例研究显示：(1) 仅最小化经济损失会系统性减少对社会脆弱区域设施的保护；(2) 沿Pareto前沿适度移动可在有限额外预期成本下大幅降低中断影响；(3) 精确分解方法能求解所有测试实例（包括广泛公式因内存受限而失败的更大规模压力测试）；(4) Lagrangian方法提供显著更快的高质量解。

Conclusion: 该研究证明在医疗设施洪水韧性投资中，经济效率与社会公平之间存在可量化的权衡关系，通过双目标优化框架可以在两者间取得良好平衡。所提出的求解方法具有较好的可扩展性，适用于实际问题规模。

Abstract: Flooding can damage healthcare facilities, interrupt local care capacity, and trigger costly patient evacuations. Addressing these risks requires long-term resilience investments under uncertainty. We develop a bi-objective two-stage stochastic optimization model that jointly determines permanent facility hardening and scenario-dependent evacuation decisions. The first objective minimizes expected evacuation, physical damage, and business-interruption costs. The second minimizes a service-disruption impact index that combines the duration and scale of service loss with a place-based social-vulnerability weight. We develop an exact Benders decomposition and a scalable Lagrangian-dual method with tailored primal recovery, embedding both within an adaptive procedure for constructing informative Pareto frontiers. A case study of 3,752 hospitals and nursing homes in Texas evaluates the framework under climate-informed tropical-cyclone flood scenarios. The results show that minimizing economic losses alone can systematically allocate less protection to facilities located in socially vulnerable areas, while moderate movement along the Pareto frontier can substantially reduce disruption impacts at limited additional expected cost. The exact decomposition solves all tested instances, including larger stress tests for which the extensive formulation becomes memory-limited, while the Lagrangian method provides substantially faster high-quality solutions.

</details>


### [218] [Directional Optimality Conditions for Optimization Problems in Asplund Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26328)
*Weihao Mao,Jane J. Ye*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在Asplund空间中为约束优化问题建立了方向性必要最优性条件，基于方向性度量次正则性推导出极限次微分形式的最优性条件，并提出了方向性度量次正则性的充分条件及其与方向性伪正规性、准正规性的关系，还针对联合约束系统提出了联合判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优性条件大多基于传统的（非方向性）度量次正则性和正规性概念，这些概念在刻画约束问题的必要最优性条件时可能过于保守或不够精细。方向性方法能够提供更精确的最优性刻画，但此前相关工作多局限于有限维空间，缺乏在无限维Asplund空间中的系统性发展。

Method: 1) 在Asplund空间中，利用方向性度量次正则性推导极限次微分形式的方向性必要最优性条件；2) 引入方向性度量次正则性的充分条件，并建立其与方向性伪正规性、准正规性之间的联系；3) 针对联合约束系统提出方向性度量次正则性的联合判据，并据此导出必要最优性条件。

Result: 获得了在Asplund空间中基于方向性度量次正则性的方向性必要最优性条件；建立了方向性充分条件与伪正规性、准正规性之间的关系；提出了联合约束系统的联合判据并得到相应的最优性条件。这些结果不仅将有限维的方向性构造推广到Asplund空间，还产生了即使在有限维中也是新的结论。

Conclusion: 本文系统地在无限维Asplund空间中发展了方向性最优性条件理论，通过方向性度量次正则性这一核心工具统一并推广了已有的方向性概念，所获得的结论即使回到有限维情形也具有新颖性，为约束优化问题提供了更精细的理论工具。

Abstract: This paper develops directional necessary optimality conditions for constrained optimization problems in Asplund spaces. Under directional metric subregularity, we first derive a directional optimality condition in terms of limiting subdifferentials. We then introduce sufficient conditions for directional metric subregularity and establish their relationships with directional pseudo-normality and quasi-normality. For systems with joint constraints, we further propose a joint criterion for directional metric subregularity and use it to obtain necessary optimality conditions. The results not only extend several finite-dimensional directional constructions to an Asplund-space setting, but also yield conclusions that are new even in finite dimensions.

</details>


### [219] [Isoperimetric problems related to extremal diameter graphs in 3D: theoretical and numerical aspects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26395)
*Beniamin Bogosel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究三维空间中极值单位直径构型（extremal unit-diameter configurations）中双对偶边长的可分泛函优化问题。作者证明收敛极值序列在合并重合点和移除悬挂顶点后保留极值几何核心，通过球面Crofton论证得到由正四面体达到的加性凹泛函的尖锐下界，并将三维Blaschke-Lebesgue面积问题精确重述为对偶边长乘积的supremal形式。数值研究覆盖所有10,644个最多16个顶点的极值构型，结合直接评估与梯度局部优化，验证每个端点包含正四面体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有限极值单位直径构型的几何结构是离散几何中的基础问题。将可分离泛函优化与Blaschke-Lebesgue问题联系起来，有助于揭示极值构型的本质性质，并为Tammes问题等经典难题提供新的分析视角。

Method: (1) 先证明收敛极值序列在合并重合顶点、移除至多关联一条直径的顶点后仍保持极值几何核心；(2) 利用球面Crofton论证推导加性凹泛函的尖锐下界；(3) 分析插入或删除悬挂顶点的影响；(4) 将双对偶边长乘积之和与3D Blaschke-Lebesgue问题建立精确supremal等价；(5) 对所有10,644个极值构型进行数值研究，结合直接评估和基于梯度的局部优化，并在顶点碰撞后重建直径图及其对偶对。

Result: 正四面体达到加性凹泛函的尖锐下界；对偶边长乘积之和给出了Blaschke-Lebesgue问题的supremal重述。数值上，每个提供的图中选出的验证端点都包含一个正四面体。计算虽未对任意固定图证明全局最大值，但为连接四面体包含性与Blaschke-Lebesgue问题的结构性猜想提供了依据。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了极值单位直径构型中双对偶边长泛函优化与Blaschke-Lebesgue问题之间的深刻联系，理论结果与大规模数值实验相互印证，为后续研究提出了正四面体包含性与Blaschke-Lebesgue问题关联的结构性猜想。

Abstract: We study optimization problems for separable functionals of the Euclidean or spherical lengths of dual edge pairs in finite extremal unit-diameter configurations in three dimensions. For a fixed diameter graph, these problems lead to nonconvex constrained optimization of the vertex coordinates. We first prove that a convergent sequence of extremal configurations retains an extremal geometric core after coincident points are merged and vertices incident to at most one diameter are removed. A spherical Crofton argument then gives a sharp lower bound for additive concave functionals, attained by the regular tetrahedron. We also analyze the effect of inserting or deleting dangling vertices. For the sum of products of spherical dual-edge lengths, we obtain an exact supremal reformulation of the three-dimensional Blaschke--Lebesgue area problem. The numerical study uses all 10,644 available extremal configurations with at most 16 vertices. We combine direct evaluation with gradient-based local optimization on each fixed graph and reconstruct the intrinsic diameter graph and its dual pairs after vertex collisions. For every supplied graph, the selected verified endpoint contains a regular tetrahedron. These computations do not certify the global maximum for any fixed graph, but they motivate structural conjectures connecting tetrahedral containment with the Blaschke--Lebesgue problem.

</details>


### [220] [Integrated Multi-Modal Transit Network Design via Dynamic Line Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26444)
*Ning Duan,Oktay Günlük,Samitha Samaranayake*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于流的混合整数规划模型，在固定运营预算下联合优化固定路线公交（线路规划与发车频率）和按需出行服务（首末公里接驳），并开发了一种带有定制化定价子问题的列生成启发式算法以实现在城市规模下的可解性。在波士顿和芝加哥的测试表明，该框架相比单一模式或多模式基准方案显著提升乘客量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定路线公共交通与按需出行服务的整合在大规模网络设计中既带来建模挑战，也提供了计算上的机遇。现有方法通常将两种模式分开优化或采用解耦策略，缺乏在统一框架下联合设计的能力，难以在等效预算约束下实现乘客量的最大化。

Method: 提出一个基于流的混合整数规划（MIP）公式，联合优化公交线路规划与服务频率，同时显式捕获按需服务的首末公里连接。为应对城市规模下的可解性挑战，开发了一种新颖的列生成启发式方案，包含针对性的定价子问题（pricing subproblems）。

Result: 在波士顿和芝加哥的网络与需求数据上进行测试。相比仅公交和仅按需服务基线，乘客量在波士顿分别提升最高20.99%和93.58%，在芝加哥分别提升最高10.63%和149.84%。相比多模式基准，在波士顿和芝加哥分别提升5.42%和5.80%。

Conclusion: （i）在统一优化框架下联合协同设计公交路线、发车频率和按需出行段，在等效预算约束下比单一模式或解耦方法产生显著更高的乘客量；（ii）所提出的公式与列生成定价方案在测试基线上实现了可解、可操作且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: The integration of fixed-route public transit and on-demand mobility services presents both a modeling challenge and a computational opportunity for large-scale network design. We propose a flow-based mixed-integer programming formulation that jointly optimizes transit line planning and service frequencies while explicitly capturing first- and last-mile connectivity via on-demand services, under a fixed operating budget. To achieve tractability at urban scale, we develop a novel column generation heuristic scheme with tailored pricing subproblems. Applied to networks and demand in Boston and Chicago, the framework yields operationally feasible designs that substantially increase demand served. Relative to transit-only and on-demand-only baselines, ridership increases by up to 20.99% and 93.58% in Boston, and by up to 10.63% and 149.84% in Chicago. Compared to a multi-modal benchmark, our approach improves ridership by 5.42% and 5.80% in Boston and Chicago, respectively. These results demonstrate that (i) joint co-design of transit routes, frequencies, and on-demand legs within a unified optimization framework yields substantially greater ridership than single-mode or decoupled approaches under equivalent budget constraints; and (ii) the proposed formulation and column generation pricing scheme admit tractable, operationally feasible, high-performing solutions relative to tested baselines.

</details>


### [221] [Stability Analysis and Data-driven Verification via Recurrent Lyapunov Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26447)
*Roy Siegelmann,Fernando Paganini,Enrique Mallada*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出"循环李雅普诺夫函数"(RLF)的概念，用循环性（每个轨迹在有限时间内返回）取代传统的前向不变性来放宽李雅普诺夫函数的条件，并开发了基于GPU的算法，仅从轨迹数据即可认证稳定性，无需显式构造李雅普诺夫函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统李雅普诺夫直接法的核心难点在于寻找李雅普诺夫函数，这需要大量的技巧或计算。关键困难在于函数的每个子水平集必须前向不变，这将其几何形状与系统轨迹耦合在一起，使构造变得极具挑战性。

Method: 1) 提出RLF概念，用循环性替代前向不变性，放宽子水平集的条件。2) 引入更强版本以获得渐近稳定和指数稳定保证。3) 给出基于范数的逆定理。4) 开发GPU并行算法，直接从轨迹数据认证稳定性，无需李雅普诺夫函数。

Result: 在温和条件下RLF能保证稳定性；认证ε邻域内的实际稳定性只需O(log(1/ε))次轨迹评估；常数随认证衰减率趋近真实衰减率而增长，揭示了固有的性能-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作通过放宽不变性为循环性，显著降低了稳定性认证的门槛，并且证明了仅从数据出发即可高效地认证稳定性，为数据驱动的控制验证提供了新的理论框架和实用算法。

Abstract: Lyapunov's direct method is a cornerstone of stability and control, but it hinges on finding a Lyapunov function, a task demanding ingenuity or computation. A key difficulty is that every sub-level set of the function must be forward invariant, coupling its geometry to the system's trajectories. We relax this by replacing invariance with recurrence: a set is recurrent if every trajectory starting in it returns within a finite time. This yields the notion of a Recurrent Lyapunov Function (RLF), whose sub-level sets need only be recurrent. We show that, under mild conditions, RLFs guarantee stability, and we introduce stronger notions yielding asymptotic and exponential stability. We also give norm-based converse theorems: under the corresponding stability conditions, any norm is an RLF for their practical versions. We then develop GPU-based algorithms that certify (practical) stability from trajectory data alone, without a Lyapunov function. Certifying stability up to an $\varepsilon$-neighborhood needs only $O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ trajectory evaluations, with constants growing as the certified decay rate nears the true one, exposing an intrinsic performance-cost trade-off.

</details>


### [222] [Galerkin approximations to the space of convex bodies by polytopes in nondegenerate V-representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26615)
*Janosch Rieger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于顶点表示的多面体来有限维逼近凸体空间的方法。对于预设的方向集合，容许点配置构成一个由有限线性不等式描述的多面体凸子锥。作者研究了参数锥的几何性质、定义不等式中的冗余性，以及凸体到多面体空间的自然投影，并导出了定量逼近估计和嵌套Galerkin序列的收敛性，最终建立了约束全局优化问题在凸体空间中有限维逼近的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸体空间是无限维的，直接处理计算困难。需要一种有限维近似方法来简化凸体空间的数值计算和优化问题，同时保持逼近精度和收敛性保证。

Method: 基于顶点表示的多面体构造有限维参数空间，通过预设方向集合定义容许点配置，形成由线性不等式描述的多面体凸子锥。研究该参数锥的几何结构、冗余不等式、自然投影，推导逼近误差估计，并构建嵌套Galerkin序列用于逼近收敛证明。

Result: 建立了有限维多面体空间对凸体空间的逼近估计，误差与方向在单位球面上的覆盖密度相关；构造了局部一致收敛到零的嵌套Galerkin序列；证明了在凸体空间中约束全局优化问题的有限维近似的收敛性。

Conclusion: 该工作为凸体空间的数值计算提供了严格的有限维逼近框架，不仅保证了逼近的定量精度，还确保了优化问题解的收敛性，为凸几何中的计算问题奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We introduce finite-dimensional approximations to the space of convex bodies based on polytopes in vertex representation. For a prescribed set of directions, the admissible point configurations form a polyhedral convex subcone of the Euclidean vector space, described by a finite system of linear inequalities. The interior of this cone contains only nondegenerate representations, in which all parameter points are distinct vertices of the represented polytope. We study the geometry of the parameter cone, redundancies in its defining inequalities, and natural projections of convex bodies onto the resulting polytope spaces. We derive quantitative approximation estimates in terms of how densely the prescribed directions cover the unit sphere and construct nested Galerkin sequences whose approximation error converges locally uniformly to zero. Finally, we use these approximation properties to establish the convergence of finite-dimensional approximations of constrained global optimization problems in the space of convex bodies.

</details>


### [223] [Quadratic Convexification of a Square Truncated by a Hyperbola](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26639)
*Yipeng Zhang,Yuyuan Ouyang,Boshi Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个由正方形被双曲线弧截断形成的紧致非凸集合G的二次凸化问题。虽然G的普通凸包是三角形，但在完全二次空间中的提升凸包保留了弯曲边界的非平凡几何结构。作者刻画了在G上非负的二次多项式锥的所有极射线，包括有界切线和双切线射线族，并通过锥对偶推导出提升凸包的精确有限半定表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非凸集合的二次凸化（quadratic convexification），这是非凸二次优化的核心问题。紧致区域与之前研究的无界乘积约束区域在几何边界接触模式上有所不同，需要新的分析。理解二次非负锥的极射线结构以及提升凸包的半定表示，对于开发更高效的全局优化算法具有重要意义。

Method: 首先研究集合G的几何结构（正方形被双曲线xy≤1截断），然后刻画在G上非负的二次多项式锥的所有极射线，具体包括参数化的有界切线和双切线射线族。利用这一极射线分类，结合锥对偶理论，推导出G的提升凸包（在完全二次空间中）的精确有限半定表示。分析框架基于作者前期关于无界乘积约束区域的工作，但针对有界几何调整了边界接触模式。

Result: 成功对所有极射线进行了分类，包括有界切线族和双切线射线族；通过锥对偶得到了提升凸包的精确有限半定表示。与之前无界区域的工作相比，有界几何产生了新的边界接触模式，导致非负二次锥具有不同的有限组织结构。

Conclusion: 紧致非凸集合G的二次凸化可以被精确地表示为有限半定集合，这证明了该区域的二次提升比普通凸包能够保留更多非凸性信息。该工作完善了有界与无界乘积约束区域的统一理论框架。

Abstract: We study the quadratic convexification of the compact nonconvex set ${G} := \left[1/2,2\right]^2 \cap \{(x_1,x_2)\in\mathbb R^2\mid x_1x_2\leq1\}, $ which is a square truncated by a hyperbolic arc. Although the ordinary convex hull of ${G}$ is a triangle, its lifted convex hull in the complete quadratic space retains the nontrivial geometry of the curved boundary. We characterize all extreme rays of the cone of quadratic polynomials nonnegative on $G$, including parameterized families of bounded tangent and bitangent rays. Using this classification and conic duality, we derive an exact finite semidefinite representation of the lifted convex hull of ${G}$. The analysis follows the general framework of our earlier work on an unbounded product-constrained region, but the bounded geometry creates new boundary-contact patterns and leads to a different finite organization of the nonnegative-quadratic cone.

</details>


### [224] [A Barrier Primal Dual Hybrid Gradient Method for Solving Linear Programming Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26667)
*Yingxin Zhou,Stefano Cipolla,Phan T. Vuong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出Barrier PDHG (BPDHG)算法，将对数障碍函数融入原始对偶混合梯度(PDHG)框架，以缓解线性规划求解中的两阶段收敛行为中漫长的活动集识别阶段导致的收敛缓慢问题。作者证明了内迭代收敛性和外序列收敛到最优解集，并将其扩展到PDLP框架形成BPDLP方法，数值实验表明该方法能有效缓解KKT残差的平台期，并提出了一个经验性的实例相关指标来预测BPDLP何时优于PDLP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDHG方法虽被验证具有两阶段收敛行为，但其中长时间的活动集识别阶段是导致收敛缓慢的主要问题，现有方法缺乏有效的缓解策略。

Method: 提出BPDHG嵌套算法，将对数障碍函数引入PDHG框架；建立内迭代收敛性及内问题误差界；证明外序列收敛到LP最优解集；将障碍技术集成到PDLP框架中形成BPDLP方法。

Result: 数值实验表明障碍修正能缓解选定实例中KKT残差的长时间平台期；提出了经验性的实例相关指标用于识别BPDLP更可能优于PDLP的LP问题。

Conclusion: 障碍函数技术能有效缓解PDHG类方法在活动集识别阶段的收敛瓶颈，且BPDLP在实际问题中具有潜在的性能优势，并为选择合适求解器提供了实用指标。

Abstract: Primal Dual Hybrid Gradient (PDHG) method has been verified to exhibit a two stage convergence behavior, in which a prolonged active set identification phase may be a major issue of slow convergence. In this paper, we propose Barrier PDHG (BPDHG), a nested algorithm which incorporates a logarithmic barrier function into the PDHG framework to alleviate this problem. We first establish convergence of the inner iterations, derive an error bound for the corresponding inner problem. Then we prove that the outer sequence generated by BPDHG approaches the optimal solution set of the LP problem we considered. Furthermore, we integrate the barrier technique into the {Primal Dual Linear Programming} (PDLP) framework to develop the corresponding Barrier PDLP (BPDLP) method. Numerical experiments show that the barrier modification can alleviate prolonged plateaus in the KKT residual on selected instances. We also investigate an empirical instance-dependent indicator for identifying LP problems on which BPDLP is more likely to outperform PDLP.

</details>


### [225] [Optimal Parameter-Free Gradient Minimization in $\ell_p$ Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26688)
*Shuting Yang,Yuning Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究ℓ_p几何中寻找小梯度查询点的一阶预言机复杂度，特别关注适应未知光滑度与距离尺度所需的信息。作者发现在严格计数的局部值与梯度模型中，若无非退化局部尺度观测，则不存在仅依赖LR/ε的有限复杂度界。作者解决了Diakonikolas关于一般ℓ_p参数无关扩展的开放问题，给出了不同p取值下的维度无关复杂度上界（1<p<2时为O_p(K̄^{1/2})，p=2时为O(K̄^{1/2})，p>2时为O_p(K̄^{p/(p+2)})），并附带对数校准代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典梯度法的大多数复杂度分析假设已知光滑常数L、初始距离R或最优值f*，但实际应用中这些参数往往未知。作者希望理解在不依赖这些先验知识（参数无关、parameter-free）的前提下，在ℓ_p几何中寻找梯度范数小的点需要多少一阶预言机调用。同时，他们希望解决Diakonikolas提出的关于一般ℓ_p空间的参数无关方法是否存在多项式复杂度界的开放问题。

Method: 作者首先给出一个一维构造，证明在严格计数模型中若无非退化局部尺度观测（如割线初始化），则任何有限查询预算都无法保证复杂度仅依赖于LR/ε。随后，在非退化割线初始化条件下，他们设计了一种参数无关方法：方法不需要知道L、R或f*，通过观测到的尺度-半径程序来拟合局部信息。对于固定p>2，作者先建立确定已知参数情形下的维度无关上界指数p/(p+2)，然后证明有限局部例程能保持该指数而无需知道L和R。复杂度分析分为不同p区间：1<p<2时复杂度为O_p(K̄^{1/2})，p=2时为O(K̄^{1/2})，p>2时为O_p(K̄^{p/(p+2)})，每种情形均附加O_p(log(e+L/M_0))的校准代价。

Result: 作者证明了：1) 在严格计数模型下，没有非退化局部尺度观测时，任何有限复杂度界都无法仅由LR/ε决定（反例中LR/ε=4但可击败任意有限预算）。2) 在非退化割线初始化下，解决了Diakonikolas的开放问题，给出了完整的一般ℓ_p参数无关上界：幂指数分别为1/2（1<p≤2时）和p/(p+2)（p>2时），其中p/(p+2)与已知的下界多项式指数相匹配。复杂度都是维度无关的。

Conclusion: 本文表明在ℓ_p几何中实现参数无关优化是可能的，但需要非退化的局部尺度观测。对于p>2，已知参数情形的上界指数p/(p+2)在未知L和R时仍然可保持，说明参数无关化不损失最优幂指数；而对于1<p≤2，复杂度可达O(K̄^{1/2})。这些结果完全解决了Diakonikolas提出的推广问题，并揭示了信息论上对局部尺度观测的必要性。

Abstract: We study the first-order oracle complexity of finding a queried point with small gradient in $\ell_p$ geometry, with particular attention to the information needed to adapt the unknown smoothness and distance scales. In the strict counted local value--gradient model, no finite complexity bound can depend only on $LR/\eps$ without a nondegenerate local scale observation: a one-dimensional construction keeps $LR/\eps=4$ while defeating every prescribed finite query budget.
  We resolve Diakonikolas's general-$\ell_p$ parameter-free extension question for every fixed $1<p<\infty$. Under a nondegenerate secant initialization, the method knows neither the smoothness constant $L$, the initial solution distance $R$, nor $f^*$, and returns a queried point $\widehat x$ with $\|\nabla f(\widehat x)\|_q\le\eps$. For fixed finite $p>2$, we first establish the dimension-free deterministic known-parameter upper exponent $p/(p+2)$ in $K=LR/\eps$, matching the published lower polynomial exponent under its horizon and dimension qualifications. The finite local routine fits the same observable scale--radius procedure, so this exponent is preserved without knowing $L$ or $R$. Writing $\Kbar=\max\{1,LR/\eps\}$, the post-initialization pair-oracle complexity is $O_p(\Kbar^{1/2})$ for $1<p<2$, $O(\Kbar^{1/2})$ for $p=2$, and $O_p(\Kbar^{p/(p+2)})$ for $p>2$, together with the additive calibration cost $O_p(\log(e+L/M_0))$ in every regime.

</details>


### [226] [When a Relaxed PEP Is Exact: The Sharp Queried-Gradient Rate of Nesterov's Fast Gradient Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26719)
*Yi Du*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文精确确定了Nesterov快速梯度法在光滑凸函数上，任意水平N≥7时最小查询梯度范数的最坏情况值，并构造了达到该上界的显式函数族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kim和Fessler通过relaxed-PEP给出了上界，并在选定水平上报告了精确插值PEP的紧数值解，但缺少在所有水平上统一成立的解析匹配族。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构造FGM特定的球面多胞体K_N，使用标准投影包络函数f_N(x)=max_{g∈K_N}{⟨x,g⟩−1/2‖g‖²}。K_N的顶点由三维种子通过一维球面锥提升生成，该提升保持所有投影不等式并使对抗维度逐水平增加。

Result: 对每个N≥7和维度d≥N−4，证明最坏情况最小梯度范数值等于L²R²/∑t_k²。构造的显式函数族中每个查询梯度范数相同，且严格达到relaxed上界。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为所有水平N≥7提供了统一的解析匹配族，解决了relaxed-PEP与精确插值PEP之间缺失的解析构造问题，并指出并非所有秩一relaxed PEP都存在此类种子。

Abstract: We determine the exact worst-case value, at every horizon $N\geq7$, of the smallest queried gradient norm generated by Nesterov's fast gradient method on smooth convex functions. Let $t_0=1$ and $t_{k+1}=(1+\sqrt{1+4t_k^2})/2$, and let $x_0,\ldots,x_N$ denote the points at which the method evaluates gradients. For every such $N$ and every dimension $d\geq N-4$, we prove \[
  \sup_{\substack{f\in\F_{0,L}(\R^d),\ x_\star\in\arg\min f
  \norm{x_0-x_\star}\leq R}}
  \min_{0\leq k\leq N}\norm{\nabla f(x_k)}^2
  =\frac{L^2R^2}{\sum_{k=0}^N t_k^2}. \] The relaxed-PEP upper bound is due to Kim and Fessler, who also reported tight numerical solutions of the exact-interpolation PEP at selected horizons. What remained missing was an analytic matching family valid uniformly over the horizon. For every $N\geq7$, we construct such a family using an FGM-specific spherical polytope $K_N$ and the standard projection-envelope function \[
  f_N(x)=\max_{g\in K_N}\left\{\ip{x}{g}-\frac12\norm{g}^2\right\},
  \qquad \nabla f_N(x)=\Proj_{K_N}(x). \] Every queried gradient has the same norm, and the vertices of $K_N$ are generated from a three-dimensional seed by a one-dimensional spherical cone lift. The lift preserves all projection inequalities and raises the adversary dimension by one at each horizon. The projection/Moreau-envelope template itself is classical; the new ingredients are the FGM-specific algebraic seed, the proof that it attains the relaxed bound, and the common-latitude lift that propagates this exactness to every $N\geq7$. We state precise hypotheses for that propagation and do not claim that every rank-one relaxed PEP admits such a seed.

</details>


### [227] [Lower Bounds for Nonconvex-PŁ Minimax Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26799)
*Siyu Pan,Jiajin Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑非凸-PL（NC-PŁ）极小极大优化中寻找值函数驻点的确定性一阶预言机复杂度，证明了在最坏情况下需要Ω(ℓΔκ/ε²)次查询的下界，与已知上界匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的工作（Yang et al., 2022）给出了NC-PŁ极小极大优化中寻找值函数驻点的确定性一阶方法的上界，但该上界中关于条件数κ的线性依赖是否不可避免尚不清楚。本文旨在通过建立匹配的下界来回答这一问题，从而刻画确定性一阶方法在该问题上的根本性限制。

Method: 作者在目标函数联合ℓ-光滑且对偶变量满足μ-PŁ条件的假设下，构造了一个最坏情况下的实例，证明任何确定性一阶方法都需要Ω(ℓΔκ/ε²)次查询才能找到满足‖∇Φ(x)‖≤ε的x。该下界在(ℓ,Δ,κ,ε)的依赖关系上与已知上界完全匹配。

Result: 证明了确定性一阶方法在NC-PŁ极小极大优化中寻找驻点的最坏情况复杂度下界为Ω(ℓΔκ/ε²)，与Yang等人2022年给出的上界在参数依赖上精确匹配，从而表明对κ的线性依赖是不可避免的。

Conclusion: 该下界结果确认了已知的确定性一阶方法上界是最优的（在参数依赖意义上），并揭示了NC-PŁ极小极大优化中条件数κ对算法复杂度的本质影响，为理解该问题的算法复杂度提供了完整的刻画。

Abstract: We study the deterministic first-order oracle complexity of finding stationary points of the value function in smooth nonconvex-Polyak-Łojasiewicz (NC-PŁ) minimax optimization. We assume that the objective is jointly $\ell$-smooth and satisfies the $μ$-PŁ condition in the dual variable, and that its value function $Φ(x):=\max_y f(x;y)$ satisfies $Φ(0)-\inf_xΦ(x)\leqΔ$. When $κ:=\ell/μ\gtrsim 1$ and $0<ε^2\lesssim\ellΔ$, we prove that every deterministic first-order method requires $Ω(\ellΔκ/ε^2)$ oracle queries in the worst case to find $x$ satisfying $\|\nablaΦ(x)\|\leqε$. This rate matches the known upper bound in its dependence on $(\ell,Δ,κ,ε)$ [Yang et al., 2022] and shows that the linear dependence on $κ$ is unavoidable for deterministic first-order methods.

</details>


### [228] [Mean-covariance turnpikes in Wasserstein distributionally robust linear-quadratic control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26986)
*Yanzhi Wu,Zhengping Ji*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究离散时间随机线性系统在经验扰动数据下的长时域Wasserstein惩罚极小极大控制问题。针对对抗性扰动分布引起时变均值与协方差动力学、使标准turnpike论证不再适用的挑战，作者通过约简的凸-凹Hamiltonian鞍点问题刻画一般非零的均值参考，证明了均值状态、伴随变量、控制、最坏情况扰动均值及闭环协方差的时域一致、双边指数turnpike估计，并构造了结合时不变仿射反馈与终端层有限时域控制的混合策略，证明其最坏情况代价间隙随终端层长度指数衰减且对时域一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域随机线性系统的鲁棒控制中，当扰动分布具有对抗性（Wasserstein惩罚）时，扰动分布诱导的时变均值与协方差动力学使经典的静态参考/平衡点（turnpike性质）难以直接刻画，增加了长时域控制的计算复杂度。

Method: 1) 通过约简的凸-凹Hamiltonian鞍点问题求解非零均值参考；2) 证明双边指数turnpike估计（覆盖均值状态、伴随、控制、扰动均值与协方差）；3) 构造混合策略：时不变仿射反馈＋终端层有限时域转向控制，利用指数衰减的代价间隙逼近最优。

Result: 证明了长时域下各关键量（均值状态、伴随、控制、最坏扰动均值、闭环协方差）多数时间停留在静态参考附近（指数turnpike）；混合策略的最坏情况代价间隙随终端层长度指数衰减且对时域一致；数值实验验证了估计及对Wasserstein惩罚参数的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该工作将turnpike性质推广到具有经验扰动数据的Wasserstein惩罚极小极大随机控制问题，为长时域鲁棒控制提供了计算上高效的分层策略（离线静态反馈＋短终端转向），并在理论上保证了近似最优性与指数收敛速度。

Abstract: We study long-horizon Wasserstein-penalized minimax control for discrete-time stochastic linear systems with empirical disturbance data, in which adversarial disturbance distributions induce time-varying mean and covariance dynamics, making standard turnpike arguments not directly applicable. For possibly uncentered data, we characterize the generally nonzero mean reference through a reduced convex-concave Hamiltonian saddle problem. We prove horizon-uniform, two-sided exponential turnpike estimates for the mean state, adjoint, control, worst-case disturbance mean, and closed-loop covariance, showing that they spend the majority of time near static references when the horizon is long. We further construct a hybrid policy combining time-independent affine feedback with finite-horizon steering over a terminal layer, proving that its worst-case cost gap decays exponentially with the terminal-layer length uniformly in the horizon, which helps reducing the computation cost for long-horizon robust controls. Numerical examples illustrate the estimates and their dependence on the Wasserstein penalty.

</details>


### [229] [Discrete eigenvalue optimization from entropic smoothing and first-order methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27024)
*Deborah Hendrych,Mathieu Besançon,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在组合和整数约束下最大化最小特征值的问题，提出了一种结合熵平滑与Frank-Wolfe方法的分支定界新方法，通过线性优化oracle利用组合结构，并在E-最优实验设计和最大代数连通性问题上与SCIP-SDP对比，在大规模及具组合结构的实例上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在组合和整数约束下最大化最小特征值是一类具有挑战性的优化问题，常见于实验设计、图优化等领域。现有的求解器（如SCIP-SDP）在处理大规模实例或具有额外组合结构的问题时效率有限，需要新的求解方法。

Method: 提出分支定界框架，核心组件包括：(1) 对最小特征值函数进行熵平滑；(2) 在约束的凹松弛上使用Frank-Wolfe方法；(3) 通过线性优化oracle利用组合结构；(4) 利用部分特征分解计算截断梯度；(5) 基于秩和特征值的剪枝策略以及基于对偶的变量固定策略。

Result: 在E-最优实验设计和最大代数连通性问题上与SCIP-SDP对比，结果表明：本方法在大规模实例和具有额外组合结构的问题上特别有效，而SCIP-SDP在中等规模且约束较简单的实例上表现更好。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种可扩展的求解组合特征值最大化问题的新范式，通过平滑与Frank-Wolfe的融合有效处理大规模问题，且在理论上建立了逼近和收敛保证；两种方法在不同场景下互补，为未来混合整数半定规划求解提供了新思路。

Abstract: We study the maximization of the minimum eigenvalue under combinatorial and integrality constraints. We propose a new approach based on branch-and-bound combines entropic smoothing of the minimum eigenvalue function and Frank-Wolfe methods over concave relaxations of the constraints, thereby exploiting combinatorial structure through linear optimization oracles. We establish approximation and convergence guarantees, including for truncated gradients computed from partial eigendecompositions, and introduce rank- and eigenvalue-based pruning and duality-based variable fixing. We evaluate the method on E-optimal experimental design and maximum algebraic connectivity problems and compare it with SCIP-SDP. The results show that our approach is particularly effective for large-dimensional instances and problems with additional combinatorial structure, whereas SCIP-SDP performs better on moderately sized instances with simpler constraints.

</details>


### [230] [Exact low-dimensional reformulations for regularized spectral approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27052)
*Shengxiang Deng,Xudong Li,Yangjing Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了正则化谱近似问题及其加权变体的精确低维重构。在无权重设置下，通过保真项的弱主化单调性实现了在奇异值上的精确重构；在加权设置下，在兼容性和同时对角化条件下建立了类似重构，并通过反例表明缺少同时对角化时该重构会失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谱近似问题在高维数据分析和信号处理中广泛出现，但直接在高维空间中求解代价高昂。寻找精确的低维重构方法可以大幅降低计算复杂度，同时保持解的精度。加权变体在更多实际场景中出现，但权重引入的复杂性使得低维重构更加困难，需要系统研究其成立条件。

Method: 本文采用理论分析方法：(1) 在无权重情况下，利用保真项的弱主化单调性证明问题可以精确地重构为仅关于奇异值的优化问题；(2) 在加权情况下，引入兼容性条件和同时对角化条件，证明在此条件下精确重构仍然成立；(3) 构造反例展示当同时对角化条件缺失时，精确重构将失败。

Result: 无权重设置下精确重构成立；加权设置下在兼容性和同时对角化条件下重构成立；通过反例证明了缺少同时对角化条件时精确重构不成立。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了正则化谱近似问题精确低维重构的成立条件，明确了加权情况下同时对角化条件的必要性，为后续研究提供了坚实的理论基础和边界条件。

Abstract: We study exact low-dimensional reformulations for a regularized spectral approximation problem and its weighted variant. In the unweighted setting, weak-majorization monotonicity of the fidelity term enables an exact reformulation over singular values. In the weighted setting, we establish such a reformulation under compatibility and simultaneous diagonalization conditions. We further show, via a counterexample, that this exact reformulation can fail in the absence of simultaneous diagonalization.

</details>


### [231] [Classification of facial exposedness of completely positive cones over symmetric cones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27062)
*Mitsuhiro Nishijima*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了对称锥上的完全正锥的面暴露性（facial exposedness），根据相关欧几里得若当代数的秩对其进行分类。当秩≤2时，锥是面暴露的；当秩≥5时，锥不是面暴露的；秩为3或4时，面暴露性不完全由秩决定，但文章给出了每种情况下的刻画。该分类结果与完全正锥的谱面性（spectrahedrality）分类一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完全正锥在优化和矩阵理论中有重要应用，而面暴露性质是凸分析中的关键几何性质，对理解锥的结构和优化算法的收敛性具有重要意义。作者希望通过秩这一简单的不变量对完全正锥的面暴露性进行系统分类。

Method: 利用欧几里得若当代数的理论框架，将完全正锥的面暴露性问题与锥的秩相关联。通过分析不同秩条件下锥的几何结构，分别证明秩≤2时的面暴露性和秩≥5时的非面暴露性，并对秩为3和4的情况给出具体刻画条件。

Result: 完全正锥在秩≥5时均不是面暴露的，在秩≤2时均为面暴露的；秩为3或4时，面暴露性取决于对称锥的具体结构，文章给出了完整的刻画。该分类与完全正锥的谱面性分类完全一致。

Conclusion: 完全正锥的面暴露性分类与谱面性分类相一致，表明这两种看似独立的性质在完全正锥语境下由相同的秩条件决定，为理解完全正锥的结构提供了统一视角。

Abstract: We classify the facial exposedness of completely positive cones over symmetric cones in terms of the rank of the associated Euclidean Jordan algebras. The completely positive cones are facially exposed when the rank is at most $2$, but are not facially exposed when the rank is at least $5$. Facial exposedness is not completely determined by the rank when the rank is $3$ or $4$, but we provide a characterization in each case. The resulting classification of facial exposedness in fact agrees with the corresponding classification of spectrahedrality for completely positive cones.

</details>


### [232] [Randomized Quasi-Gauss--Newton Methods for Solving General Nonlinear Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27084)
*Chengchang Liu,Luo Luo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Gram矩阵近似的随机拟高斯-牛顿方法，用于求解一般非线性方程组，涵盖欠定与过定两种情形。欠定情形下方法具备局部超线性收敛性；过定情形下方法对条件数不敏感地收敛到非线性最小二乘问题的驻点，克服了现有拟牛顿方法主要局限于方阵系统且需初始Jacobian估计足够接近的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拟牛顿方法大多只关注方阵系统（即方程数与未知数相等的情况），且要求初始Jacobian估计必须足够接近真实Jacobian，这在实际问题中往往难以满足。对于欠定和过定的非线性方程组，缺乏有效的局部收敛性保证。

Method: 基于Gram矩阵近似的随机拟高斯-牛顿方法。通过近似Gram矩阵来构造迭代更新，分别针对欠定与过定两种设定设计相应的算法框架，并利用随机化技术降低计算成本。

Result: 欠定情形：方法达到局部超线性收敛到最优解；过定情形：方法达到局部条件数无关（condition-number-free）的收敛到非线性最小二乘问题的驻点。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法拓展了拟牛顿方法的应用范围，不再局限于方阵系统，同时放松了对初始Jacobian估计接近性的要求，为一般非线性方程组的求解提供了更稳健且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper considers the local convergence for solving general nonlinear equations. We establish randomized quasi-Gauss--Newton methods based on the approximation of the Gram matrix, addressing both the underdetermined and the overdetermined settings. For the underdetermined case, we show that our methods achieve local superlinear convergence to the optimal solution. For the overdetermined case, we show that our methods achieve local condition-number-free convergence to the stationary point of the nonlinear least-square formulation of the problem. In contrast, existing quasi-Newton methods mostly focus on square systems and additionally require the initial Jacobian estimate to be sufficiently close to the exact one.

</details>


### [233] [Co-Optimized Generation, Transmission, and Storage Expansion: System Value and Optimal Duration of Pumped-Storage Hydropower](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27222)
*Rafael Benchimol Klausner,Rafael Kelman*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个发电-输电-储能联合扩展规划框架，其中储能时长作为优化变量而非预设参数。模型为滚动时域两阶段随机线性规划，以五年为阶段、小时级分辨率、十种场景，应用于巴西互联系统2030-2050年。结果显示，允许36个抽水蓄能候选（4-144小时时长）参与竞争时，模型选择建设31.2 GW/755 GWh抽水蓄能而非电池储能，使2050年系统年化成本降低6.8%，运营成本降低23.5%。核心结论是：将储能时长固定为单一值会低估系统最优储能需求并高估对火电的残余需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩展规划模型在优化前就固定储能时长，只能决定储能功率大小而无法优化放电持续时间，这可能导致储能时长配置次优，进而影响整个系统最优容量组合的准确性。本文旨在将储能时长作为优化结果而非输入参数，以更真实地评估不同时长储能技术的经济价值。

Method: 构建滚动时域两阶段随机线性规划模型，每个五年阶段包含全年小时级分辨率（8760小时/年）模拟，涵盖十种一致的入流、风能和太阳能场景。规划框架中，多种时长的储能候选（抽水蓄能4-144小时共九种时长、电池储能4小时）按年化成本竞争，储能时长组合成为优化输出。应用于巴西互联系统2030-2050年，对比有/无抽水蓄能两种情形。

Result: 有抽水蓄能时，模型建设31.2 GW/755 GWh抽水蓄能且不建电池储能，以12小时时长为主导，另有4.9 GW的72小时机组。无抽水蓄能时，需建设38 GW/152 GWh电池储能及额外7.8 GW燃气机组（多为开式循环调峰机组）。抽水蓄能可使2050年年化系统成本降低50亿美元/年（6.8%），运营成本降低23.5%，热力发电量减少34 TWh/年，长期边际成本降低20%，2035年弃电率从8.4%降至3.2%。

Conclusion: 固定单一储能时长会系统性低估系统最优储能需求并高估残余火电需求。尽管具体数值因巴西国情而特定，但这一机制具有普适性：扩展规划应允许多种储能时长同时竞争，使储能时长组合成为优化结果，才能准确评估储能对降低系统成本和火电依赖的贡献。

Abstract: Expansion planning models usually fix storage duration before optimization, setting how much storage power to build but not for how long it can discharge. We present a generation-transmission-storage expansion framework in which candidates of many durations compete on annualized cost, making the duration mix an optimization outcome. It is a rolling-horizon, two-stage stochastic linear program, each five-year stage is a full year at hourly resolution under ten coherent inflow, wind, and solar scenarios. We apply it to the Brazilian Interconnected System over 2030-2050, where demand roughly doubles to 1,716 TWh/year and variable renewable energy (VRE) supplies most new capacity. Two cases are compared: 36 pumped-storage hydropower (PSH) candidates over four subsystems and nine durations (4-144 h) alongside 4-h battery energy storage systems (BESS), and BESS alone.
  With PSH available, the model builds 31.2 GW / 755 GWh of PSH and no BESS, dominated by 12-h capacity with 4.9 GW of 72-h units. Without PSH it builds 38 GW / 152 GWh of BESS and 7.8 GW more gas-fired capacity, mostly open-cycle peakers. PSH lowers 2050 annualized system cost by US$ 5.0 billion/year (6.8%), operating cost by 23.5%, thermal generation by 34 TWh/year, and long-run marginal cost by 20%; 2035 VRE curtailment falls from 8.4% to 3.2%. The magnitudes are specific to Brazil, but the underlying mechanism is general: limiting storage candidates to a single duration understates the system's optimal energy-storage requirement and overstates its residual need for thermal capacity.

</details>


### [234] [Asymptotic consensus and flocking under decaying persistent excitation on rooted digraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27263)
*Chiara Cicolani,Elisa Continelli,Cristina Pignotti*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有非通用交互、时间延迟和通信失败可能性的一阶与二阶对齐模型，将已有结果从强连通交互图推广到仅需有根交互图，并放宽了持久激励条件，允许固定长度时间区间内的交互量随时间多项式衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究（如文献[17]）对一阶和二阶对齐模型的分析依赖较强的连通性假设和激励条件，本文旨在探讨在更弱、更现实的条件下（交互图仅有根、通信随时间衰减、存在延迟和失败），集体行为（共识与集结）是否仍能涌现。

Method: 对一阶Hegselmann-Krause型模型，在通信权重衰减指数与根节点最大距离之间建立适当条件，证明渐近收敛到共识；对二阶Cucker-Smale模型，在影响函数衰减的额外假设下，建立渐近集结结果。分析方法基于将交互图假设从强连通放宽为有根。

Result: 证明了一阶模型在多项式衰减通信下仍可渐近共识，二阶模型在影响函数附加衰减条件下仍可渐近集结，表明即使通信逐渐弱化且不要求交互图强连通，集体行为仍能涌现。

Conclusion: 集体行为（共识与集结）在渐进弱化的通信和更弱的图假设下依然可以产生，本文推广了已有结果，增强了模型在实际场景中的适用性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate first- and second-order alignment models with non-universal interaction, time delays and possible communication failures, extending the results in [17] to interaction digraphs that are only assumed to be rooted and to a weaker Persistence Excitation Condition. In particular, we allow the amount of interaction over time intervals of fixed length to decay polynomially in time.
  For the first-order Hegselmann-Krause type model, we prove asymptotic convergence to consensus under a suitable condition relating the decay exponent of the communication weights to the maximal distance from the root. For the second-order Cucker-Smale model, we establish asymptotic flocking under an additional assumption on the decay of the influence function. These results show that collective behavior can still emerge under progressively weakening communication and without requiring strong connectivity of the interaction graph.

</details>


### [235] [Marine Autonomous Vehicle Fleet Scheduling to Maximise Scientific Impact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27271)
*Mehdi El Krari,Jonathan Smith,Maria Fox*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型，用于自动化优化海洋自主航行器（MAVs）的部署调度，涵盖电池容量、数据采集时间窗等严格约束，并创新性地整合传统船舶航线以支持中途换电或加速转运。实验证明模型可扩展到数十至数百辆MAV的规模，同时提供可视化工具支持战略决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋科学界日益依赖MAVs采集环境数据，但随着作业规模扩大，人工路由和调度大型自主舰队变得极其复杂且耗时，亟需自动化优化方案。

Method: 构建MILP模型，纳入电池容量和时间窗约束，以最大化数据采集量、最小化部署车辆数和能耗为多目标；创新地将传统船舶航线整合进框架，使MAVs能在任务中途获得电池更换或加速转运支持。

Result: 计算实验表明模型高度可扩展：数十辆MAV的路径规划问题在数秒内求解，数百辆规模也仅需几分钟。此外，模型还可作为"假设情景"模拟工具，并生成可视化结果增强可解释性。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅能用于实际运营调度，还可作为稳健的仿真工具评估参数变化对部署策略的影响，并通过可视化支持利益相关者的战略决策。

Abstract: The marine science community increasingly relies on Marine Autonomous Vehicles (MAVs) to collect the critical environmental data required to understand global ocean systems. However, as these operations scale, manually routing and planning large autonomous fleets becomes exponentially complex and time-consuming. To address this, we propose a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) model designed to automate and optimise MAV deployment schedules. The model accounts for strict operational constraints, including battery capacities and time windows for data collection, while aiming to maximise total data collection and minimise both the number of deployed vehicles and their energy consumption. A key novelty of this framework is integrating conventional ship itineraries, allowing MAVs to support vessels with mid-mission battery swapping or accelerated transit between waypoints. Computational experiments demonstrate that the model is highly scalable, solving routing problems for fleets of dozens of MAVs in seconds, and scaling to hundreds of vehicles in only a few minutes. Beyond operational scheduling, the framework serves as a robust simulation tool for evaluating 'what-if' scenarios and analysing the impact of varying parameters on deployment strategies. Finally, the solution generates a suite of visualisations designed to enhance explainability and support strategic decision-making for stakeholders.

</details>


### [236] [When MILP Beats QP: Piecewise-Linear Reformulations of Sequentially Coupled Bilinear Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27312)
*Quentin Ploussard,Maris Usis,Oluwabunmi Iwakin,Matija Pavičević*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对双线性项导致的非凸二次规划问题，提出基于连续分片线性（CPWL）逼近的高效MILP公式，引入三种MILP公式（Triangle、Square、DC），定义了Sequentially Coupled Bilinear Programs (SCBP)模型类别，并通过数值实验证明紧凑的"Square"公式在长序列SCBP实例上性能优于直接QP求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双线性项在数学建模中广泛存在，产生非凸二次规划（QP），难以全局最优求解。虽然CPWL逼近可将其转化为MILP，但域划分的几何构造严重影响求解效率，现有方法缺乏对公式的系统比较和误差的严格分析。

Method: 1) 利用单位双线性函数的对称性构造可达到任意精度的CPWL逼近；2) 解析推导逼近的最大和平均误差；3) 构建并比较三种MILP公式（Triangle、Square、DC公式）；4) 形式化定义SCBP模型类别；5) 进行大规模数值实验对比各公式与直接QP求解器的性能。

Result: 数值实验表明，紧凑的"Square"MILP公式在长序列SCBP实例上计算性能最优，使成熟的开源MILP求解器能够有效超越直接的QP求解器。

Conclusion: 通过合理设计CPWL逼近的域划分和MILP公式，可以显著提高双线性规划的求解效率，特别是"Square"公式为长序列耦合系统提供了一种优于QP方法的实用替代方案。

Abstract: The presence of bilinear terms in mathematical modeling generally yields nonconvex quadratic programs (QPs) that remain computationally challenging to solve to global optimality. While continuous piecewise linear (CPWL) approximations can reformulate these nonlinearities into mixed-integer linear programs (MILPs), the geometric construction of the domain partition heavily dictates the resulting solver efficiency. In this paper, we present efficient MILP formulations for approximating bilinear terms and rigorously evaluate their computational merits against direct QP solvers. First, we introduce CPWL approximations with arbitrary high degrees of accuracy that explicitly account for and exploit the inherent symmetries of the unit bilinear function. Second, we analytically establish the exact maximum and average approximation errors of the CPWL approximations. Third, we construct and compare three distinct MILP formulations (a ``Triangle'', a ``Square'', and a difference-of-convex ``DC'' formulation) of the CPWL functions. Fourth, we formalize a highly relevant class of optimization models, Sequentially Coupled Bilinear Programs (SCBP), where variables represent system states and state changes. Finally, through extensive numerical experiments, we demonstrate that our compact ``Square'' MILP formulation achieves superior computational performance on SCBP instances with long sequence horizons, allowing mature open-source MILP solvers to effectively outperform QP solvers.

</details>


### [237] [UGM: A Unified Framework and New Perspectives for Accelerated Gradient Methods in Smooth and Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27368)
*Danqing Zhou,Shiqian Ma,Junfeng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出统一加速梯度方法框架UGM，涵盖多种加速和传统梯度方法。该框架可解释为heavy-ball方法与vanilla梯度下降的混合组合，并通过Lyapunov函数建立统一收敛分析，进而开发出利用梯度与迭代变量内积关系的增强型加速算法，在二次优化和逻辑回归中表现优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典加速梯度方法（如heavy-ball、Nesterov加速方法）虽各有优势，但缺乏统一的框架来揭示其内在联系和设计原理，难以系统性地理解和改进。

Method: 提出UGM统一框架，将迭代更新解释为heavy-ball方法（快速但不稳定）与vanilla梯度下降（保守但稳定）的混合组合，实现加速与稳定性的权衡；利用Lyapunov函数建立统一收敛分析，并基于梯度信息与迭代变量的内积关系优化迭代更新，开发增强型加速梯度算法。

Result: 在无约束二次优化和逻辑回归上的大量数值实验表明，所提出的算法在典型结构条件下相比现有基线方法具有更优的性能。

Conclusion: 经典加速梯度方法本质上是在快速而不稳定的heavy-ball动力学中融入保守的梯度下降步骤，UGM框架统一了这一类方法，并据此衍生出性能更优的增强型加速算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a unified framework for accelerated gradient methods, dubbed UGM, which subsumes a wide range of accelerated and conventional gradient-type methods designed for minimizing $L$-smooth and $μ$-strongly convex functions. We demonstrate that the iteration update of the proposed framework can be intrinsically interpreted as a hybrid combination of the heavy-ball method and vanilla gradient descent. This interpretation reveals that classical accelerated gradient methods essentially integrate a conservative gradient descent step into the fast yet unstable heavy-ball dynamics, which achieves a favorable trade-off between acceleration and stability. We further establish a unified convergence analysis using Lyapunov functions. Guided by our analysis, we develop a family of enhanced accelerated gradient algorithms that leverage the inner product relationship between gradient information and iterative variables to optimize iterative updates. Extensive numerical experiments on unconstrained quadratic optimization and logistic regression validate that the proposed algorithms achieve superior performance compared with existing baseline methods under typical structural conditions.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [Production Scheduling Identification: An Inverse Optimization Approach for Industrial Load Modeling Using Smart Meter Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26485)
*Ruike Lyu,Hongye Guo,Qinghu Tang,Qixin Chen,Chongqing Kang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出PSI（生产调度识别）方法，一种基于逆优化的工业负荷建模方法，利用智能电表数据在无法获取工业用户私有生产数据的情况下，识别生产调度参数，实现对工业负荷的准确建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求侧响应能有效管理电力系统供需平衡，但工业用户的生产数据属私有信息无法直接获取，导致基于状态任务网络（STN）等通用模型的负荷建模参数无法设定，难以准确建模工业负荷。

Method: 提出基于逆优化的生产调度识别（PSI）方法，利用工业用户的智能电表数据识别生产调度参数；结合改进的STN模型实现，并提出实用算法获得有效解。

Result: 数值测试表明，PSI仅需21天的每小时智能电表数据即可识别钢铁粉末厂和水泥厂的模型参数；与直接访问私有数据建立的精确模型相比，建模误差分别不超过8.5%和5.2%。

Conclusion: PSI在不获取工业用户私有数据的前提下，能够以可接受的精度完成工业负荷建模，为需求侧响应中工业负荷的准确建模提供了有效且经济可行的解决方案。

Abstract: To cost-effectively manage the supply-demand balance of the power system, the flexibility of industrial users could be harnessed through demand-side response. To minimize the negative impact on the production of industrial users during demand-side response, general-purpose models such as the state-task network (STN) are widely used to model the energy-consuming constraints of industrial production processes. However, the required model parameters cannot be set because the required data are privately owned by industrial users and are not directly available, hindering the accurate modeling of industrial loads. In this paper, we propose production scheduling identification (PSI), an inverse-optimization-based approach for industrial load modeling under incomplete information. In PSI, industrial users' smart meter data are used to identify production scheduling parameters, thus addressing the problem of accurate load modeling when private data are unavailable. We implemented PSI with a modified STN and proposed a practical algorithm to obtain an effective solution. Numerical tests showed that PSI can identify the model parameters of a steel powder plant and a cement plant with acceptable accuracy, using only 21 days of hourly smart meter data. Compared with accurate models established with direct access to private data, the modeling error does not exceed 8.5% and 5.2%, respectively.

</details>


### [239] [Real-Time Operation Strategy of Virtual Power Plants With Optimal Power Disaggregation Among Heterogeneous Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26486)
*Qixin Chen,Ruike Lyu,Hongye Guo,Xiangbo Su*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种虚拟电厂（VPP）提供电网频率调节的最优运行策略，通过优先利用低成本资源并考虑资源的时间耦合特性，实现异构资源的实时最优功率分配。同时提出快速分解算法以消除对优化求解器的依赖，仅需毫秒级在线计算时间，降低了运行成本并提高了利润。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VPP聚合需求侧灵活资源提供频率调节时，需要在实时将功率调整量分解到内部异构资源中。这一过程面临三大挑战：资源的时间耦合特性、调节信号的不确定性、以及快速响应的要求。现有研究依赖比例分解等启发式方法，未能充分利用多资源的异构性来实现最优分配。

Method: 1）提出VPP最优运行策略，利用异构资源的互补特性，优先使用低成本资源，同时考虑时间耦合约束；2）提出快速分解算法，减少在线部署的计算开销，避免依赖优化求解器。

Result: 在由温控负荷和工业生产过程组成的VPP案例研究中，验证了所提策略降低了运行成本并提高了VPP利润，在线计算时间仅为毫秒级。

Conclusion: 该工作有助于更好地挖掘需求侧灵活性潜力，为VPP提供频率调节服务时实现高效、经济的最优功率分配提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The virtual power plant (VPP) can aggregate flexible resources on the demand side to provide frequency regulation for the grid, helping address the supply-demand balance challenges. When deploying regulation, the VPP disaggregates the requested power adjustment in real time among its internal heterogeneous resources. Achieving optimal power disaggregation in this process is challenging due to the temporal coupling characteristics of the resources, the uncertain regulation signals, and the requirement for fast response. Therefore, existing research relies on heuristic methods, such as proportional disaggregation, and fails to leverage the heterogeneity of multiple resources. Here, we propose an optimal operation strategy for VPPs to provide regulation, exploiting the complementary characteristics of heterogeneous resources by prioritizing the use of low-cost resources while considering temporal coupling. To reduce the computational overhead of online deployment, we further propose a fast disaggregation algorithm to eliminate the reliance on optimisation solvers. We conducted case studies on the operation of a VPP composed of resources including thermostatically controlled loads and industrial production processes. The results verified the reduced operation cost and increased profit of the VPP under the proposed strategy, with only milliseconds of online computation time. We believe that our work can help better exploit demand-side flexibility.

</details>


### [240] [Efficient Scheduling of Discrete Industrial Processes Through Continuous Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26487)
*Ruike Lyu,Xiangbo Su,Ershun Du,Hongye Guo,Qixin Chen,Chongqing Kang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出连续资源任务网络（cRTN）模型，通过使用连续变量表示生产任务和进度，为离散工业过程（如炼钢）建立统一且计算高效的数学规划模型，相比传统RTN模型具有更少的二元变量、更快的求解速度和更好的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RTN模型虽然广泛应用于工业过程的技术约束建模，但包含大量二元变量，且对灵活和非灵活过程采用不同的公式化方法，导致计算效率低、适用性受限，难以满足工业需求响应的计算要求。

Method: 提出cRTN建模方法，用连续变量表示生产任务和进度，将资源平衡、任务执行、等待时间限制和生产目标等技术约束整合为统一的、计算上有利的公式化表达。

Result: 基于炼钢厂数值测试表明：cRTN在典型场景下比传统模型快10倍；随着批次规模增大仍保持可解性（传统模型则因规模膨胀而无法求解）；通过解决传统模型的舍入误差问题，实现了能源成本的降低。

Conclusion: cRTN作为一种统一的连续建模框架，在保持精度的同时显著提升了工业过程模型的计算性能和可扩展性，为工业需求响应提供了更高效的建模基础。

Abstract: The resource-task network (RTN) model has been widely applied to represent the technical constraints of complex industrial processes (IPs) such as steel-making, providing the basis for industrial demand response. However, the legacy RTN model contains numerous binary variables and applies different formulations for non-flexible and flexible processes, restricting its computational efficiency and applicability. To systematically improve the computational performance of IP models, we propose continuous RTN model (cRTN), a novel modeling approach that uses continuous variables to represent production tasks and progresses, which are then integrated into unified as well as computationally favorable formulations for the technical constraints in discrete IPs, including resource balance, task execution, waiting time limits, and production targets. Compared to the legacy models, cRTN features fewer binary variables, shorter solving time, and better scalability while maintaining the same accuracy. Numerical tests based on a steel plant demonstrate that cRTN is in typical cases 10 times faster than legacy models and remains tractable with increasing batch sizes, which in legacy models leads to larger problem scales and infeasible solving time. cRTN also achieves a reduction in energy costs by resolving the issue of rounding errors reported in legacy models.

</details>


### [241] [Energy as a Concealable State in Adversarial UAV Patrolling: Formulation, an Energy-Security Threshold, and the Limits of Self-Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26518)
*Sai Krishna Reddy Mareddy*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究能量受限的对抗性巡逻问题，其中电池受限的无人机需要在图上巡逻并定期返回基地充电，同时防御战略性攻击者。论文将交互形式化为零和部分可观测随机博弈，发现深度Q学习和神经虚构自博弈都无法达到稳定均衡，并通过结构分析证明安全性与能量预算单调相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性巡逻工作未考虑巡逻者的能量约束，而能量感知巡逻又忽视自利对手的存在。本文指出剩余能量是隐藏状态：攻击者虽不能直接观察电池，但可通过轨迹推断充电时机，从而识别脆弱窗口。这一空白需要形式化建模并评估现有算法是否适用。

Method: 将巡逻问题形式化为零和部分可观测随机博弈（POSG）；采用独立深度Q学习与神经虚构自博弈两种方法训练；同时利用独立于学习动力学的结构分析来研究能量预算对安全性的影响。

Result: 两种学习方法均无法在问题规模下达到稳定均衡，训练中联合成功防御率从约0.25下降至约0.09。结构分析表明可达安全性随能量预算单调增加：低于阈值时安全为零，预算充足时可达约0.7，确立能量预算是可防御性的主要决定因素。

Conclusion: 能量预算从根本上决定可防御性，现有深度强化学习方法在此类部分可观测场景下不稳定。论文提出后续研究问题：推理型攻击者是否会在充电窗口集中攻击，以及防御者能否学习欺骗性充电时机来封锁该窗口。

Abstract: We study energy-constrained adversarial patrolling on a graph, in which a battery-limited UAV defends a cluster of high-value targets against a strategic attacker who chooses when and where to strike. Unlike prior adversarial patrolling, the patroller must periodically return to a base to recharge; unlike prior energy-aware patrolling, it faces a self-interested adversary. Our central observation is that the remaining energy is a hidden state: the attacker never observes the battery directly, but observes the patroller's trajectory and can infer when a recharge excursion, and thus a vulnerability window, is imminent. We formalize the interaction as a zero-sum partially observable stochastic game and report a negative result on the solver side: neither independent deep Q-learning nor Neural Fictitious Self-Play reaches a stable equilibrium at this scale; each improves transiently and then collapses, with the co-trained thwart rate falling from about 0.25 to about 0.09 over training. Using a structural analysis independent of the learning dynamics, we show that achievable security rises monotonically with the energy budget, from zero below a threshold to about 0.7 when the budget is ample, establishing the energy budget as the primary determinant of defensibility. We set out the program the model is built to answer: whether an inference-capable attacker concentrates its successful strikes in the recharge window, and whether the defender can learn deceptive recharge timing to keep that window closed.

</details>


### [242] [Fusion Based Dynamic Platform Magnetic Compensation Beyond the Tolles Lawson Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26525)
*Rong Yang,Yaakov Bar-Shalom*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段航空磁补偿框架：首先通过增广线性化模型进行传感器标定参数估计（避免经典带通滤波的信息损失），然后利用预估计参数，开发无需地图的卡尔曼滤波融合（KFF）算法，从受平台干扰的多传感器测量中实时动态估计外部磁场并补偿平台干扰。在公开DAF-MIT MagNav数据集上验证，将平均补偿误差从359.2 nT降至12.3 nT，并克服了现有方法的非因果局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Tolles-Lawson（TL）框架主要关注补偿模型参数的估计，但较少关注动态外部磁场的实时估计。这限制了其在磁导航（MagNav）、磁异常检测（MAD）等需要实时动态磁场跟踪应用中的性能。

Method: 1. 第一阶段：构建增广线性化模型用于基于传感器的标定参数估计，避免经典带通滤波（BPF）带来的信息损失。2. 第二阶段：利用预估计参数，开发一种无地图的卡尔曼滤波融合（KFF）算法，直接基于受平台干扰污染的多传感器测量数据，动态估计外部磁场并补偿平台干扰，实现实时联合动态估计与多传感器补偿，无需参考位置数据或先验异常图。

Result: 在公开DAF-MIT MagNav数据集上验证，该框架将平均补偿误差从359.2 nT降低到12.3 nT，同时克服了现有方法非因果的局限性，并在传感器通道严重受损的情况下保持了优异的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段标定-补偿框架有效弥合了TL框架中参数估计与实时外部磁场动态估计之间的鸿沟，实现了无需地图、无需参考位置的实时多传感器磁补偿，显著提升补偿精度与鲁棒性，为MagNav、MAD等应用提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Aeromagnetic compensation for airborne platforms is essential for real-time tracking of the dynamic external magnetic field free from platform interference, enabling robust magnetic navigation (MagNav), magnetic anomaly detection (MAD), and other geophysics applications. While the classical Tolles-Lawson (TL) framework focuses extensively on estimating compensation model parameters, it pays less attention to the real-time estimation of the dynamic external magnetic field. To bridge this gap, this paper proposes a two-stage calibration and compensation framework. First, an augmented linearized model is developed for sensor-based calibration parameter estimation, avoiding the information loss inherent in classical band-pass filtering (BPF). Second, utilizing these pre-estimated parameters, a map-less Kalman Filter Fusion (KFF) algorithm is developed to dynamically estimate the external field and compensate the platform interference directly from multi-sensor measurements corrupted by platform interference. The second step enables real-time joint dynamic estimation and multi-sensor compensation without requiring reference position data (which runs counter to the MagNav purpose) or prior anomaly maps. Validated on the public DAF-MIT MagNav dataset, the framework overcomes the non-causal limitations of existing approaches, reducing average compensation error from 359.2~nT to 12.3~nT while maintaining exceptional robustness against heavily corrupted sensor channels.

</details>


### [243] [Barrier Function Conformal Safety Clearance Certification with CVaR for Driving Trajectory Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26533)
*Pei Yu Chang,Qadeer Ahmed*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，用于评估自动驾驶运动规划器所选轨迹的实际安全净空（safety clearance），并通过可微分的分离轴障碍物边界（SAB）margin 对精确的有符号OBB安全净空进行确定性下界估计。框架结合了计划时的CVaR尾部风险采样和事后选择的共形校准（conformal calibration），在可交换驾驶会话上提供统计有效性保证。在nuPlan 300会话基准测试中，该方法在10%目标错误覆盖下将共形校正从1.43m降至0.03m，并将非负安全净空证书率从68.7%提升至87.3%，所有统计量上的精确净空覆盖率保持在93.3%-96.7%，高于90%目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶运动规划器虽然会生成并选择候选轨迹并考虑与周围智能体的交互，但这些评估并不能认证所选轨迹的实际安全净空。规划器评估的时间余量与真实安全净空之间存在差距，需要一个框架来校准这种差距并提供具有统计保证的安全认证。

Method: 该方法包括：(1) 使用可微分的分离轴障碍物（Separating Axis Barrier）margin，以确定性方式下界精确的有符号OBB安全净空；(2) 在计划时分别使用名义预测和采样下尾CVaR（Conditional Value-at-Risk）评估margin；(3) 通过事后选择共形校准（post-selection conformal calibration）在可交换的驾驶会话上吸收预测和采样误差，从而提供独立于预测器正确性的统计有效性保证。

Result: 在冻结的300会话nuPlan研究上，使用原生Predictive Driver Model (PDM)闭环提案进行评估。在10%目标错误覆盖下，采样下尾CVaR将共形校正从1.43m减少到0.03m，并将非负安全净空证书率从68.7%提高到87.3%。在所有评估统计量中，精确净空覆盖率保持在93.3%-96.7%，高于90%的目标。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将统计证书与安全margin连接起来，通过CVaR采样显著减少共形校正量并提高安全证书率，同时保持了高于目标水平的覆盖率。这为自动驾驶安全认证提供了一种兼具确定性下界和统计有效性保证的方法。

Abstract: Autonomous driving motion planners generate and select candidate trajectories while accounting for interactions with surrounding agents. However, these evaluations do not certify the actual safety clearance of the selected trajectory. The framework evaluates the trajectory selected by ant planners and calibrates the gap between its plan time margin and realized safety clearance. A differentiable separating axis barrier margin deterministically lower bounds exact signed oriented-bounding-box (OBB) safety clearance, connecting the statistical certificate to safety margin. At plan time, the margin is evaluated using either a nominal prediction and sampled lower tail Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR), while post-selection conformal calibration over exchangeable drive sessions absorbs prediction and sampling errors. Conformal calibration provides statistical validity independently of predictor correctness. The method is evaluated on a frozen 300 session nuPlan study using native Predictive Driver Model (PDM) Closed loop proposals. At 10% target miscoverage, sampled lower CVaR reduces the conformal correction from 1.43m to 0.03m and increases the rate of nonnegative safety clearance certificates from 68.7% to 87.3%. Across all evaluated statistics, exact-clearance coverage remains above the 90% target at 93.3--96.7%.

</details>


### [244] [Camera Calibration Using Inaccurate and Asynchronous Discrete GPS Trajectory from Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26548)
*R. Yang,Y. Bar-Shalom,H. A. J. Huang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究固定相机标定问题，利用无人机GPS轨迹估计相机姿态角（偏航、俯仰、翻滚）。针对GPS高度偏差、时间不同步、离散插值三大挑战，提出了一种基于迭代最小二乘（ILS）的最大似然估计器，同时估计GPS高度偏差、时间偏移和相机姿态角偏差。仿真结果表明该算法达到CRLB下界，并推荐了可达到良好标定精度（测量误差标准差的14%）的无人机轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用GPS记录的无人机轨迹作为固定相机标定的地面真值存在三大挑战：GPS高度数据精度差且存在未知偏差；GPS接收机与相机之间时间不同步存在未知时间偏移；GPS轨迹是时间离散的，需要精确插值且速度估计也必不可少。这些挑战使得传统标定方法无法直接适用。

Method: 将问题建模为参数估计问题，估计向量包含GPS高度偏差、时间偏移以及相机偏航、俯仰、翻滚偏差。针对离散非同步GPS轨迹，开发了基于迭代最小二乘（Iterated Least Squares）算法的特殊最大似然估计器。此外，论文还分析了标定精度对无人机轨迹的依赖，并提出了推荐的无人机飞行轨迹方案。

Result: 仿真测试验证了算法性能，估计结果满足Cramer-Rao Lower Bound（CRLB），归一化估计误差平方在统计上可接受。推荐的无人机轨迹可实现标定后残余偏差仅为测量误差标准差的14%，满足高精度标定要求。

Conclusion: 论文提出的方法有效解决了GPS轨迹作为标定地面真值的三大挑战，实现了高精度相机姿态角标定。由于相机测量误差通常很小，该高精度标定方法能够确保标定后的残余偏差相对于测量误差标准差不显著，且通过轨迹设计可进一步提升标定精度。

Abstract: This paper considers a stationary camera calibration problem, which estimates the camera orientation angles yaw, pitch and roll, using a drone trajectory recorded by a GPS. There are three challenges in using a GPS trajectory as ground truth for camera calibration. One, the altitude of GPS data is inaccurate with an unknown bias. Two, the GPS receiver and camera are not time synchronized, and there is an unknown time offset between the two systems. Three, the GPS trajectory is time-discrete and accurate interpolation is needed. This is actually an estimation problem since velocity is also needed. To address the first two challenges, we formulate the problem as a parameter estimation problem to estimate a vector consisting of the GPS altitude bias and time offset in addition to the camera yaw, pitch and roll biases. We then develop a special maximum likelihood estimator using the Iterated Least Squares algorithm which can work with a non-synchronized time-discrete GPS trajectory for the third challenge. Since the camera measurement errors are usually small, this requires a high calibration accuracy so that the residual bias error following the calibration should not be significant compared to the measurement error standard deviation. The calibration accuracy depends highly on the drone trajectory. This paper also recommends an appropriate drone trajectory which can yield a good calibration accuracy, namely, 14\% of the measurement error standard deviation. Simulation tests are conducted to demonstrate the algorithm performance. The estimation results meet the Cramer-Rao Lower Bound (CRLB) since the Normalized Estimation Error Squared w.r.t.\ the CRLB is statistically acceptable.

</details>


### [245] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Guidance Law with Target Input Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26551)
*Minho Jang,Minjeong Kim,Sungsu Park*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视角的非线性模型预测控制制导方法（MPCG），用于配备捷联导引头的导弹。该方法利用视角及其导数作为状态变量，避免了弹体速率耦合和寄生反馈问题，并通过LGR伪谱法将最优控制问题离散化为非线性规划求解。仿真结果表明，在满足视场角和加速度约束的同时，MPCG在单机动场景下优于传统纯比例导引，具有更好的稳定性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统比例导引（PNG）需要视线角速率测量值，而捷联导引头系统无法直接获取这些测量值。因此需要一种更适合捷联导引头测量特性的制导方法，以克服测量限制并提高制导性能。

Method: 1) 将视角及其导数作为状态变量构建MPCG框架，消除弹体速率耦合；2) 将制导问题表述为连续时间最优控制问题，采用Legendre-Gauss-Radau伪谱法离散化并转化为非线性规划求解；3) 加入显式视场角（FOV）和加速度约束；4) 采用自适应扩展卡尔曼滤波器（AEKF）结合交互式多模型（IMM）框架估计预测时域第一步的目标加速度。

Result: 在包含俯仰/偏航平面摆动和桶滚机动的单机动场景仿真中，MPCG实现了可靠拦截并满足操作约束，在稳定性和鲁棒性方面优于纯比例导引（PPNG）。

Conclusion: MPCG为受捷联导引头测量限制的现代导弹制导系统提供了一种实用且有效的解决方案，在稳定性和鲁棒性上优于传统PPNG。

Abstract: This paper presents a look angle-based nonlinear model predictive control guidance (MPCG) method for missiles equipped with strapdown seekers. Conventional proportional navigation guidance (PNG) requires line-of-sight (LOS) rate measurements, which are not directly available in strapdown systems.
  MPCG instead employs look angles and their derivatives as state variables, eliminating body-rate coupling and associated parasitic feedback. The guidance problem is formulated as a continuous-time optimal control problem (OCP), discretized via the Legendre-Gauss-Radau pseudo-spectral method (LGRPM), and solved as a nonlinear program (NLP) incorporating explicit field-of-view (FOV) and acceleration constraints. Target acceleration at the first step of the prediction horizon is estimated using an adaptive extended Kalman filter (AEKF) integrated with an interacting multiple model (IMM) framework.
  Simulation results under single-maneuver scenarios, which include pitch and yaw plane weaving as well as barrel-roll maneuvers, demonstrate that MPCG achieves reliable interception while satisfying operational constraints, outperforming pure PNG (PPNG) in stability and resilience. This indicates that MPCG offers a practical and effective solution for modern missile guidance systems constrained by seeker measurement limitations.

</details>


### [246] [Current-Limiting Control for Fault Ride-Through of LLC-based Solid-State Transformer in Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26595)
*Haoyu Wang,Chi Zhang,Mafu Zhang,Rudy Wang,Peter Barbosa*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种嵌入在固态变压器（SST）DC-DC级控制中的协调直流负载故障限流与恢复策略，通过调节开关频率和占空比在微秒级内将直流电流限制到指定值，并在故障隔离后通过斜坡恢复避免冲击电流，无需额外硬件成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固态变压器作为配电网与数据中心之间的接口广泛应用于灵活功率流动和快速动态响应，但当负载支路发生短路故障时，电压源型DC-DC级会因故障电流被迫停机。需要限流策略来防止设备损坏和级联停电，同时为保护装置提供足够电流以动作于故障支路。

Method: 以LLC谐振变换器为例研究故障机理，实现故障检测框架；提出闭环电流控制器，通过增大开关频率并调节占空比，在微秒级内将直流电流限制到指定值；故障隔离后采用斜坡恢复阶段恢复直流母线，避免冲击电流。

Result: 在LLC变换器样机上的实验验证了所提限流策略的可行性，实现了更快、更低成本的故障响应。

Conclusion: 所提出的策略直接嵌入SST DC-DC级控制中，避免额外硬件开销，适用于高韧性的数据中心供电架构。

Abstract: Solid-State Transformers (SSTs) are increasingly proposed as the interface between distribution grids and data centers due to flexible power flows and fast dynamic response. However, when a short-circuit fault occurs in a load branch, the SST with a voltage-source-type DC-DC stage is forced to shut down due to fault currents. Therefore, current-limiting strategies are strongly needed to prevent catastrophic equipment damage and cascading blackouts by instantly restricting massive current spikes and offering sufficient currents for protection devices to act at the faulted branch. This paper proposes a coordinated DC load fault-tolerant current-limiting and recovery strategy embedded directly in the control of the SST DC-DC stage, avoiding additional hardware cost. Specifically, the fault mechanism of an example LLC resonant converter is studied. Accordingly, a fault detection framework is implemented, a closed-loop current controller is proposed to limit the DC current to a designated value within microseconds by surging the switching frequency and adjusting the duty cycle, and a ramped recovery stage will then restore the DC bus after the fault isolation without inrush currents. Experiments on an LLC converter prototype have verified the feasibility of the proposed current-limiting strategy, enabling faster and lower-cost fault response suitable for resilient data center power architectures.

</details>


### [247] [Joint Spectrum and Airspace Resource Optimization for Low-altitude Wireless Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26830)
*Yafei Guo,Ziye Jia,Lei Zhang,Jingxian Liu,Qiuming Zhu,Qihui Wu*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段耦合的顺序框架，协调频谱分配与空域规划以构建高效的低空空域走廊。通过将空域离散化为数字网格，设计了约束VCG升价拍卖机制分配频谱资源，并提出了联合频谱与空域资源分配算法以最小化走廊总通行成本，仿真结果验证了其优于基线算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空无线网络是保障无人机安全高效运行的重要平台，但由于频谱和空域资源有限，在无碰撞的前提下高效完成无人机飞行任务面临巨大挑战，亟需协同优化频谱分配与空域规划。

Method: 1) 将低空空域离散化为数字网格，障碍物建模为不可渗透单元；2) 构建最小化空中走廊总通行成本的优化问题；3) 设计约束VCG升价拍卖机制分配频谱资源；4) 提出联合频谱与空域资源分配算法求解优化问题。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提出的算法相比基线算法能够实现更低的总通行成本，验证了该框架在低空空域走廊构建中的有效性与优越性。

Conclusion: 通过频谱分配与空域规划的耦合协同，该框架能够有效利用有限资源构建高效低空空域走廊，为无人机任务的无碰撞高效执行提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Low-altitude wireless networks have emerged as a promising platform for enabling the safe and efficient operation of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). However, due to the limited spectrum and airspace resources, it is challenging to efficiently accomplish UAV flight tasks without collisions. In this paper, we propose a sequential framework with two coupled stages that coordinates spectrum allocation and airspace planning to construct efficient low-altitude air corridors. Specifically, the low-altitude airspace is discretized into a set of digital grids, where obstacles are modeled as impermeable units. Then, we formulate an optimization problem to minimize the total traversal cost of air corridors, which is challenging to solve due to the tight coupling between spectrum allocation and path planning.Therefore, we first design a constrained Vickrey-Clarke-Groves (VCG) ascending auction mechanism to allocate the spectrum resources. Then, we propose a joint spectrum and airspace resource allocation algorithm to minimize the total traversal cost of air corridors. Finally, simulation results show that the proposed algorithms achieve lower total costs than the baseline algorithms.

</details>


### [248] [Comparative Assessment of Frequency Scans using EMT and RMS Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26851)
*Clea Bürgel,Gustavo Valverde,Gabriela Hug*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文比较了同步发电机、跟网型和构网型逆变器在dq电磁暂态模型与RMS模型下的频率扫描结果，发现RMS模型仅在低频有效，其中电流控制型构网逆变器比电压控制型更难用RMS表征，但加入内环电压控制可提升精度。文章还研究了发电机和电网阻抗对公共耦合点系统总阻抗的影响，并将振荡模态与零点分别关联到系统阻抗第一、第二奇异值的峰值与谷值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在逆变器主导的电力系统中，逆变器和电网阻抗的频率扫描对小信号稳定性和系统强度评估至关重要，因此需要对比不同机组模型（电磁暂态与RMS）在频率扫描中的有效性和准确性。

Method: 在Simulink中，对同步发电机、跟网型逆变器和构网型逆变器分别建立全dq电磁暂态模型和RMS模型，在相同额定容量和运行点下进行频率扫描，并结合时域仿真验证；同时分析发电机和电网阻抗对公共耦合点系统总阻抗的影响，将系统模态与奇异值关联分析。

Result: RMS模型仅在低频范围内有效；电流控制型构网逆变器比电压控制型更难用RMS准确表征，但加入内环电压控制环可提高RMS精度；系统阻抗的第一奇异值峰值对应振荡模态，第二奇异值谷值对应系统零点。

Conclusion: RMS模型在低频分析中适用，但用于高频或电流控制型构网逆变器时需要谨慎；内环电压控制有助于改善RMS建模精度，同时系统阻抗奇异值可有效关联振荡模态与系统零点，为小信号稳定性分析提供依据。

Abstract: Frequency scans of inverter and grid impedances are crucial for assessing small-signal stability and system strength in inverter-dominated power systems. This paper compares frequency-scan results for several generating units, including a synchronous generator, grid-following, and grid-forming inverters, using fully dq electromagnetic transient and root-mean-square (RMS) models in Simulink. The scans are performed for the same device rating and operating point. Frequency scans and time-domain simulations show that the RMS models are valid only at low frequencies. We find that the current-controlled grid-forming inverter is more difficult to represent in RMS than the voltage-controlled grid-forming inverter. However, including the inner voltage-control loops improves the accuracy of the RMS representation. The paper also investigates the influence of generator and grid impedance on the total system impedance seen from the point of common coupling and relates the oscillatory modes and zeros of the system to peaks and dips in the first and second singular values of the system impedance, respectively.

</details>


### [249] [Towards Safe Reinforcement Learning with Reduced Conservativeness: A Case Study on Drone Flight Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26852)
*Loizos Hadjiloizou,Michael C. Welle,Hang Yin,Danica Kragic*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将形式化方法与强化学习相结合的框架，通过在线数据精化扰动模型，并使用基于zonotope的可达性分析来评估学习控制器的安全性，从而在保证系统安全的前提下减少形式化方法的保守性，并在真实无人机峡谷飞行实验中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将形式化方法融入强化学习可以兼顾形式化保证的稳健性与RL的适应性和学习能力，但需要精心设计以平衡安全与探索。现有方法往往过于保守，限制了探索效率，因此需要一种能减少保守性同时保证安全性的框架。

Method: 提出一种较少限制的框架：1) 利用在线收集的数据精化扰动模型，降低形式化方法的保守性；2) 使用计算高效的zonotopic可达性分析评估基于学习的控制器的安全性，以支持实时实现。

Result: 在真实无人机峡谷飞行实验中，无人机受到未知外部扰动，框架在线学习扰动并调整安全保障。结果表明该框架能够在保证系统安全的前提下，实现较少限制的在线训练。

Conclusion: 该框架有效平衡了安全与探索之间的矛盾，通过在线扰动模型精化和高效可达性分析，实现了在不牺牲安全性的前提下减少形式化方法的保守性，验证了其在真实世界环境中的可行性。

Abstract: Incorporating formal methods into reinforcement learning (RL) has the potential to result in the best of both worlds, combining the robustness of formal guarantees with the adaptability and learning capabilities of RL, though careful design is needed to balance safety and exploration. In this work, we propose a framework to mitigate this loss of exploration while still allowing for the safety of the system to be ensured. Specifically, we introduce a less restrictive method that can reduce the conservativeness of formal methods by refining a disturbance model using online collected data and it evaluates the safety of a learning-based controller, using computationally efficient zonotopic reachability analysis for the safety analysis to facilitate a real-time implementation. We validate the framework in a real-world drone flight through a canyon, where the drone is subjected to unknown external disturbances and the framework is tasked with learning those disturbances online and adjusting the safety guarantees accordingly. The results show that the framework enables a less restrictive online training of learning-based controllers without compromising the safety of the system.

</details>


### [250] [Data-driven Koopman mode approximation: A neural power iteration algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26943)
*Guillaume O. Berger,Raphaël M. Jungers*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于神经网络的数据驱动算法，用于近似非线性动力系统Koopman算子的主导本征函数（模式），通过幂迭代方案直接学习主导模式，避免显式构造算子投影，从而克服维度灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习Koopman主导模式有助于将非线性动力学在高维空间中近似为线性动力学，便于控制与分析。然而，使用神经网络等高表达力模板进行模式近似时面临维度灾难问题。现有方法虽通过学习小字典函数规避此问题，但需要"防坍缩机制"来保证字典的表达力。

Method: 提出一种幂迭代方案，直接学习主导Koopman模式，无需显式构造Koopman算子在函数模板上的投影。方法完全数据驱动，仅需采样状态转移数据。理论保证方面，结合神经正切核（NTK）定理，证明了在样本量和网络宽度增大的条件下收敛性。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法能够获得主导模式的精确且光滑的近似，同时避免了传统技术（如扩展动态模态分解EDMD）的局限性。

Conclusion: 该方法的优势在于通过幂迭代设计天然收敛到投影Koopman算子的主导模式，不需要额外的防坍缩机制，从而在保持表达力的同时克服了维度灾难。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel data-driven algorithm to approximate the dominant eigenfunctions (aka.~modes) of the Koopman operator of nonlinear dynamical systems using neural networks. The relevance of learning the dominant Koopman modes is to approximate nonlinear dynamics by linear ones in a lifted space, thereby enabling simplified control and analysis. To fight the curse of dimensionality arising from using expressive templates (here neural networks) for the mode approximation, the proposed method leverages a power-iteration scheme that directly learns the dominant Koopman modes without explicitly constructing the projection of the Koopman operator on the template of functions. Our approach connects to other approaches in the literature that avoid the curse of dimensionality by learning small dictionaries of functions, but differs from them in that we do not require ``anti-collapse mechanisms'' to ensure that the learned dictionary is expressive enough to approximate the Koopman operator since our power-iteration scheme is designed to converge toward the dominant modes of the projected Koopman operator. The approach is fully data-driven, requiring only sampled state transitions. Theoretical guarantees are provided, showing convergence under increasing sample size and network width (in connection with the neural tangent kernel theorem). Numerical experiments demonstrate that the method achieves accurate and smooth approximations of dominant modes while avoiding the limitations of traditional techniques such as extended dynamic mode decomposition.

</details>


### [251] [Energy-Neutral Coverage Optimization by Joint Deployment and Scheduling in Ambient IoT Devices with Directional Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26944)
*David E. Ruíz-Guirola,Samuel Montejo-Sánchez,Richard Demo Souza,Onel L. A. López*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究环境物联网(A-IoT)设备在能量采集和占空比限制下的联合部署与感知调度问题，提出了四种策略（网格部署+静态占空比、策略梯度强化学习RL、混合整数线性规划LP、以及LP+RL混合方法），实验表明RL和LP+RL方法在覆盖率上优势显著（最高2倍性能提升），且LP+RL通过结构化初始化可将离线优化时间减少高达10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A-IoT设备依赖能量采集和占空比循环来维持运行，与传统常开传感器网络有本质区别，这从根本上改变了协作感知的方式。因此需要研究如何在能量约束下优化A-IoT设备的部署位置和感知调度，以最大化感知覆盖。

Method: 研究四种解决方案：(i) 网格部署+静态占空比（基线）；(ii) 集中式策略梯度RL，从网格部署出发学习能量感知的设备重定位和占空比策略；(iii) 混合整数线性规划，耦合静态部署设计与占空比分配；(iv) 混合LP+RL方法，结合基于优化的初始化与基于学习的精细化调优。

Result: 在低能量采集和中能量采集场景下，LP+RL和RL策略一致优于网格基线和LP方法，平均有效覆盖率最高提升2倍。独立LP方法因在紧能量约束下保守的静态占空比分配而受限。此外，LP+RL的结构化初始化显著加速收敛，相比独立RL将总离线优化时间减少最多10倍。

Conclusion: 混合LP+RL方法在性能（覆盖率）和效率（收敛速度）之间取得了最佳平衡，既优于纯学习方法的收敛慢问题，又优于纯优化方法的性能上限限制，是A-IoT设备部署与感知调度的有效方案。

Abstract: Ambient IoT (A-IoT) devices rely on energy harvesting and duty cycling to sustain operation, thereby fundamentally changing collaborative sensing compared with traditional always-ON sensor networks. In this paper, we study the joint deployment and sensing scheduling of A-IoT devices equipped with directional sensing. We explore four solution strategies: (i) a grid deployment with static duty cycling, (ii) a centralized policy-gradient reinforcement learning (RL) approach that begins with a grid deployment and learns energy-aware device relocation and duty-cycling policies, (iii) a mixed-integer linear programming (LP) approach that couples static deployment design with duty-cycle allocation, and (iv) a hybrid LP+RL that combines optimization-based initialization with learning-based refinement. Using representative A-IoT use cases, we evaluate coverage as a function of device density, field-of-view, and maximum feasible duty cycle, determined by harvested energy and device consumption. Numerical results indicate that the proposed LP+RL and RL policies consistently outperform both the grid baseline and the LP-based method, achieving up to 2x higher mean effective coverage in low and medium energy harvesting (EH) regimes. In contrast, the standalone LP method remains limited by its conservative static duty cycle allocation under tight EH constraints. Moreover, the structured initialization of the LP+RL method substantially accelerates convergence, reducing the total offline optimization time by up to 10x compared to the standalone RL.

</details>


### [252] [Mitigating Forced Oscillations in Power Systems via Data-Enabled Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26975)
*Soraya Daabak,Verena Häberle,Gabriela Hug,Gustavo Valverde*

Main category: eess.SY
