Our results indicate that our method serves as an efficient pooling layer, enhancing the ability of GNNs to capture hierarchical structural information in networks.

</details>


### [320] [Diffusion Policies for Short-Horizon Planning in Robot Crowd Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27158)
*Wendong Li,Jochen Garcke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PDPO（规划扩散策略优化）框架，用于机器人人群导航。它利用扩散策略生成短期动作块，先通过无碰撞演示离线预训练，再使用PPO在线微调（将去噪过程视为内部决策过程）。执行时生成五步动作块并以滚动时域方式应用。此外，作者发现基准测试中存在评估缺陷（智能体可能绕过人群离开有效区域），因此引入将边界违规视为碰撞的新设置。实验表明PDPO在成功率上优于强基线，消融实验证明动作块在修改后的有界基准中尤为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在每一步仅输出单一反应性动作，难以表征密集、动态、多模态人机交互下的多样化短期避障策略，限制了人群导航的安全与效率。

Method: 提出离线到在线的强化学习框架PDPO：1) 使用扩散策略生成短时程动作块（5步）；2) 先在无碰撞演示上预训练，再用PPO在线微调，将去噪过程作为内部决策过程；3) 执行时以滚动时域方式应用动作块；4) 引入将边界违规视为碰撞的新评估设置以修复基准缺陷。

Result: PDPO在成功率上优于强基线；消融实验表明动作块在修改后的有界基准中尤其重要，验证了多步动作规划对短期避障策略多样性的价值。

Conclusion: 扩散策略结合动作块规划能有效提升人群导航的性能，同时指出并修复了现有基准中的评估伪影，为人群导航的强化学习研究提供了更公平的评估框架。

Abstract: Robot crowd navigation requires safe and efficient decision-making under dense, dynamic, and multimodal human--robot interactions. Existing reinforcement-learning methods typically output a single reactive action at each timestep, which limits their ability to represent diverse short-term avoidance strategies. We propose Planning Diffusion Policy Optimization (PDPO), an offline-to-online reinforcement-learning framework that uses a diffusion policy to generate short-horizon action chunks for crowd navigation. PDPO is first pretrained on collision-avoidance demonstrations and then fine-tuned online with PPO by treating the denoising process as an internal decision process. During execution, the policy generates a five-step action chunk and applies it in a receding-horizon manner. Furthermore, we observe an evaluation artifact in common crowd-navigation benchmarks: without explicit boundary constraints, learned agents may leave the valid domain and bypass dense crowds. To address this, we introduce a setting in which boundary violations are treated as collisions. Experiments show that PDPO obtains an improved success rate over strong baselines, and ablations demonstrate that action chunks are especially important for the modified bounded benchmark.

</details>


### [321] [TraceBench: Controlled Evaluation of LLM Agents for Time-Series Root-Cause Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27182)
*Tommaso Bendinelli,Artur Dox,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceBench，一个基于仿真的框架，用于生成受控的根因归因任务，以系统评估LLM智能体在时间序列异常检测与根因分析中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多地被应用于真实世界系统中的异常检测和根因分析，但在受控条件下其性能尚未被系统评估，缺乏标准化的评测基准。

Method: TraceBench通过仿真物理动力学系统生成受控的根因归因任务。每个任务中，智能体接收由物理动力学系统仿真产生的时间序列观测，并需判断系统参数是否在仿真过程中被改变以及具体是哪个参数被改变。作者从三个可解释的机械系统中生成任务，并在受控实验条件下系统评估四个LLM智能体。

Result: 实验发现：智能体从领域上下文中获益显著；智能体主要通过数值控制台输出而非可视化来探索数据；当要求智能体生成将每个时间序列样本映射到预测根因标签的Python脚本时，其表现通常比直接提交预测更差。

Conclusion: TraceBench为LLM智能体的时间序列根因分析提供了首个受控评测框架，揭示了LLM智能体在分析动力学系统时间序列数据时的行为模式与局限，并公开了数据集、轨迹、实验结果和排行榜以促进后续研究。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to anomaly detection and root-cause analysis in time-series observations collected from real-world systems; however, their performance on these tasks has not been systematically evaluated under controlled conditions. We introduce TraceBench, a simulation-based framework for generating controlled root-cause attribution tasks. In each generated task, an agent receives time-series observations produced by simulating a physical dynamical system and must determine whether a system parameter was altered during the simulation and, if so, which one. Using TraceBench, we generate tasks from three interpretable mechanical systems and systematically evaluate four LLM agents across controlled experimental conditions, yielding new insights into how these agents analyze time-series observations from dynamical systems. Our results show that agents benefit substantially from domain context and explore data primarily through numerical console output rather than visualizations. We also find that agents generally perform worse when required to produce a Python script that maps each time-series sample to a predicted root-cause label than when they submit predictions directly. We release our datasets, agent trajectories, experimental results, and a leaderboard on our website, tracebench.github.io.

</details>


### [322] [When Interference Graphs Evolve: Doubly Robust Estimation of Dynamic Peer Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27187)
*Xiaojing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在交互图动态演化背景下估计同伴效应的受控对比框架，并开发了 DynaNet-DR 双重稳健估计器，该估计器结合时间因子化倾向得分与归一化增强项，在半合成基准和 MathOverflow 观测研究中展现了良好的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当交互图随时间演化时，同伴效应的估计变得困难，因为预分配网络历史、动态同伴暴露和后分配网络变化具有不同的因果作用，需要一种能够清晰区分这些作用的框架。

Method: 引入受控对比框架，对潜在结果按自身处理、时间聚合同伴暴露和后分配演化摘要进行索引，由此定义自身处理对比、同伴暴露对比、受控网络演化对比和联合受控对比。开发 DynaNet-DR 估计器，结合时间因子化倾向得分与归一化增强，并加入代表性得分预测、固定裁剪和有限样本稳定化。

Result: 在一致性、摘要充分性、序列可交换性、正性、干扰收敛和弱依赖等条件下，典型估计器在结果回归或倾向得分估计任一一致时均保持一致。半合成基准和 MathOverflow 研究显示良好的估计精度。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种非中介分解的对比方式来估计动态网络中的同伴效应，DynaNet-DR 在多种设定下具有双重稳健性，为动态交互图中的因果推断提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Peer effects are difficult to estimate when interaction graphs evolve because pre-assignment network history, dynamic peer exposure, and post-assignment network change have distinct causal roles. We introduce a controlled contrast framework that indexes potential outcomes by own treatment, temporally aggregated peer exposure, and a post-assignment evolution summary. Differences between the resulting means define own-treatment, peer-exposure, controlled network-evolution, and joint controlled contrasts rather than a mediation decomposition. We develop the Dynamic Network Doubly Robust estimator, DynaNet-DR, which combines a temporally factorized propensity with normalized augmentation. Under consistency, summary sufficiency, sequential exchangeability, positivity, nuisance convergence, and weak dependence, its canonical estimator is consistent when either the outcome regression or the propensity estimator is consistent. The reported implementation adds representative-score prediction, fixed clipping, and finite-sample stabilization. Semi-synthetic benchmarks on fixed real temporal graph sequences show favorable estimation accuracy among methods targeting the full profile. These benchmarks assess summary-indexed contrasts rather than counterfactual edge generation, and the MathOverflow study is an observational illustration under the stated assumptions.

</details>


### [323] [Common Geodesics Do Not Guarantee Fisher Consistency of the Structured SVM: Minimal Counterexamples and a Tree-Metric Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27203)
*Jintao Fei,Jiangying Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了结构化支持向量机中Fisher一致性所需条件的充分性问题。发现"任务损失是度量且每个输出三元组有公共测地点"这一已知必要条件，对于标准的逐坐标argmax解码器而言并不充分。作者完全分类了带正权重的树度量：argmax一致性成立当且仅当树是路径。分支树上的失败仅限于边界分布，所有满支撑分布下树度量仍保持argmax性质。此外给出了满支撑反例的最小规模（5个输出，K2,3图）及三维Hamming立方体的反例，所有最优性声明均有精确的原对偶证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化支持向量机（SSVM）的Fisher一致性至关重要，但现有理论仅提供了必要非充分条件。作者旨在揭示已知的"公共测地点"条件在坐标wise argmax解码器下并不充分，填补了理论空白，并给出精确的充分必要条件分类。

Method: 通过构造精确反例（四输出单位星图、K_m,n图族、三维Hamming立方体）证明条件不充分；利用对偶理论和原对偶证书验证最优性；对带正权重的树度量进行完全分类，区分边界分布与满支撑分布两种情形。

Result: （1）四输出单位星图存在精确最优分数向量，其所有最大化者均严格非Bayes，且4个输出是满足条件度量中的最小反例；（2）树度量的argmax一致性当且仅当树是路径；（3）分支树的失败仅限边界分布，满支撑分布下argmax性质保留；（4）5个输出是满支撑反例的最小规模，K2,3是最小的K_m,n族成员；（5）三维Hamming立方体也有满支撑反例。

Conclusion: 证明了"公共测地点"条件在argmax解码器下不充分，揭示了嵌入能保证校准链接存在但无法保证指定argmax链接在代理风险最小化器上有效的"解码器间隙"。工作为结构化预测中解码器选择提供了重要的理论指导，表明度量结构本身不足以确保一致性，解码器的具体形式至关重要。

Abstract: A known necessary condition for Fisher consistency of the structured support vector machine requires the task loss to be a metric for which every output triple has a common geodesic point. We show that this condition is not sufficient for the canonical coordinate-wise argmax decoder. A four-output unit star admits an exactly optimal score vector whose maximizers are all strictly non-Bayes, and four outputs are minimal among metrics satisfying the condition. We then completely classify positively weighted tree metrics whose vertex set is the output space: argmax consistency holds if and only if the tree is a path. The failure on branching trees is confined to boundary distributions; every tree retains the argmax property at every full-support distribution. Among metrics satisfying the common-geodesic condition, five outputs are necessary and sufficient for a full-support counterexample; $K_{2,3}$ is the smallest member of an infinite $K_{m,n}$ family. We additionally give a full-support counterexample for the three-dimensional Hamming cube. All optimality claims have exact primal-dual certificates. The counterexamples expose a concrete decoder gap: in this polyhedral setting, an embedding can guarantee the existence of a calibrated link without validating a prescribed argmax link on every surrogate-risk minimizer.

</details>


### [324] [Profit based evaluation of machine learning for nitrogen recommendations in winter wheat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27205)
*Xulong Wang,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究冬小麦氮肥施用量的决策问题。标准UK建议不响应价格变化，而机器学习通常以预测精度为评判标准，但预测准确不意味着推荐更盈利。作者提出直接以利润损失（基于实测产量响应曲线）来评分氮肥建议，在892条响应曲线上构建测试平台，扫描氮肥/谷物价格比覆盖所有价格场景。结果发现机器学习作为预测器失败，没有模型能在农户容差内恢复最佳施氮量，且在正常价格下都输给标准建议。真正的收益在于简单修正步骤：将利润损失削减四分之一，在第二个试验点无需再训练即可降低43%损失。混合标准建议加阻尼修正可消除偏差并削减罕见大损失。同样的价格扫描也为减排定价，成本接近当前碳价。结论是机器学习应作为标准建议的利润评分修正，而非替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氮肥施用量在季节前设定，面临未知价格和天气。标准UK建议不响应价格波动，而近年来价格大幅波动使最盈利施氮量变动数十公斤/公顷。现有机器学习方法通常以预测精度评价，但预测精度不直接等同于推荐更盈利，存在评估指标与真实目标错配的问题。

Method: 提出以利润损失（profit forgone）直接评分氮肥建议的新评估框架。在892条UK长期试验的产量响应曲线上构建测试基准，通过扫描氮肥/谷物价格比覆盖所有价格场景，系统评估标准建议、机器学习预测器及其修正策略的表现。

Result: 机器学习作为预测器在所有场景下均失败，没有模型能在农户容差内恢复最佳施氮率，基准噪声显示任何模型都无法实现。正常价格下所有模型在利润上均输于标准建议。但简单的模型后修正步骤可将利润损失削减25%，在第二站点无需再训练即可降低43%损失。混合标准建议加阻尼修正可消除偏差并削减罕见大损失。价格扫描为减排定价的成本接近当前碳价。

Conclusion: 机器学习在氮肥推荐中的正确角色是作为标准建议的利润评分修正器（correction），而不是替代品（replacement）。修正后的框架在利润、跨站点泛化性和风险控制（削减大损失）上都优于纯ML预测，并可为减排决策定价。

Abstract: Nitrogen rates for winter wheat are set before the season, under unknown prices and weather. The standard UK advice does not respond to prices, yet recent price swings moved the most profitable rate by tens of kilograms per hectare. Machine learning is often proposed as the fix. However, it is usually judged on prediction accuracy, and accurate prediction does not by itself make the recommended rate more profitable. Our insight is to score nitrogen advice directly by the profit it forgoes on measured yield response curves. We build a test bench on 892 such curves from two long running UK experiments, and sweep the nitrogen to grain price ratio to cover all price scenarios. On this bench, machine learning fails as a predictor. No model recovers the best rate within farm tolerance, and the benchmark noise shows none can. At normal prices, every model also loses to the standard advice on profit. The gain sits elsewhere. A simple correction step applied after the model cuts profit losses by a quarter, while better models and extra features give no gain. The same frozen correction cuts losses by 43% at the second site without any retraining. A hybrid of standard advice plus a damped correction removes bias and trims rare large losses. The same price sweep also prices emission cuts, at a cost comparable to current carbon prices. Machine learning therefore pays as a profit scored correction to standard advice, not as its replacement.

</details>


### [325] [HALO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Language-Aligned IMU Foundation Model for Open-Set Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27233)
*Zihan Ding,Liyu Zhang,Xiaomin Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了HALO，一个面向IMU人体活动识别的领域特定基础模型，通过两阶段训练框架（异质性感知自监督预训练 + 同义词感知软对比学习的语言对齐）解决传感异质性和跨数据集泛化问题，在10个数据集上训练、7个留出数据集上评估，以约35M参数超越五个SOTA基线，零样本开放集准确率提升13.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAR领域缺乏能跨受试者、设备和活动泛化的统一模型。两大核心挑战阻碍了基础模型的训练：一是传感异质性（采样率、通道配置、传感器位置差异），二是对未见活动和标签词汇的泛化能力差。

Method: 两阶段训练框架。Stage 1：异质性感知自监督预训练，包含自适应池化分词、通道独立特征提取、上下文传感条件注入（将自然语言传感器描述嵌入每个通道）。Stage 2：通过同义词感知软对比学习将IMU编码器与文本嵌入对齐，利用余弦相似度检索实现开放集识别，无需逐数据集分类器。

Result: 在10个公共HAR数据集训练、7个留出数据集评估，HALO在全部8个聚合指标上超越五个SOTA基线；在基线匹配输入下仍领先4个设置中的3个。仅用约35M可训练参数（比MOMENT的341.2M少10倍），零样本开放集准确率提升13.7个百分点。在两个分布偏移严重的数据集上所有模型包括HALO的零样本性能均崩溃。

Conclusion: HALO证明了领域特定的IMU基础模型可以在参数规模远小于通用时序基础模型的情况下，通过异质性感知和语言对齐实现更强的跨数据集泛化与开放集识别能力，但极端分布偏移仍是开放挑战。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using inertial measurement units (IMUs) enables a wide range of applications, yet the field still lacks a unified model that can generalize across diverse subjects, devices, and activities. Training such a model is difficult due to two key challenges: sensing heterogeneity -- differences in sampling rates, channel configurations, and sensor placements -- and poor generalization to unseen activities and label vocabularies. We introduce HALO (Heterogeneity-Aware Language-aligned Open-set model), a domain-specific IMU foundation model that addresses both challenges through a two-stage training framework. Stage 1 pretrains the IMU encoder with heterogeneity-aware self-supervised learning, including adaptive-pooling tokenization, channel-independent feature extraction, and contextualized sensor conditioning that injects natural-language sensor descriptions into each channel embedding. Stage 2 aligns this IMU encoder with text embeddings via synonym-aware soft contrastive learning, enabling open-set recognition via cosine-similarity retrieval without per-dataset classifiers. Trained on 10 public HAR datasets and evaluated on 7 held-out datasets, HALO outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines on all 8 aggregate metrics, and still leads on 3 of 4 settings under baseline-matched inputs. Despite using only ~35M trainable parameters -- 10x fewer than the latest foundation model MOMENT (341.2M) -- HALO improves zero-shot open-set accuracy, measured over all 87 training labels, by 13.7 percentage points. On two further datasets with severe distribution shift, every model including HALO collapses zero-shot. A video demonstration of HALO's performance in real world is available at https://youtu.be/rooVKragtFU

</details>


### [326] [Importance Scoring of Transformer Attention Heads in Learning Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27241)
*Ahmad Jad Allah,Kazi F. Akhter,Md. Kamrozzaman Bhuiyan,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将注意力头重要性评分指标应用于表格数据上的多头Transformer模型，通过40个多样化表格数据集的实验，验证了基于重要性分数逐步移除低重要性注意力头时模型性能最稳健，而优先移除高重要性头会最大程度降低分类性能。研究发现重要注意力头分散在各层中，且与图像和语言领域不同，表格数据中注意力头的重要性因数据集模式和特征空间不同而变化显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型计算密集且不透明，需要通过分析数据变换方式来更好地理解和优化模型。虽然深度Transformer在计算机视觉和自然语言处理领域已被广泛研究，但在表格数据上的应用相对缺乏探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，为表格数据上的Transformer模型提供可解释性和效率优化方法。

Method: 提出一种重要性评分指标用于解释表格数据上的多头Transformer模型。在40个多样化表格数据集上开展实验，通过逐步移除不同重要性等级的注意力头，评估模型性能的变化情况。同时分析六个注意力层中各注意力头的重要性分数分布，并对比不同数据集之间的差异。

Result: 在72.5%的实验案例中，当逐步移除最低重要性分数的注意力头时，模型对性能下降的抵抗性最强；而首先移除最重要的注意力头会导致分类性能最大程度下降。重要注意力头分散在各层中，没有一致的层特定趋势，且与图像和语言领域不同，表格数据中注意力头的重要性在不同数据集之间差异显著。

Conclusion: 所提出的重要性评分能够提升Transformer架构的效率和冗余性，为表格数据上的Transformer模型提供了一种有效的解释和优化工具。源代码已公开，便于后续研究者使用和扩展。

Abstract: Computationally demanding and opaque deep learning models can be better understood and optimized by analyzing how they transform data. While deep transformers have been widely studied in computer vision and natural language processing, their application in tabular data remains relatively underexplored. This paper presents one of the first applications of an importance-scoring metric to interpret multi-head transformer models in learning from tabular data. Experiments conducted on 40 diverse tabular datasets demonstrate robustness to head drops based on the proposed head importance score. In 72.5\% of experimental examples, the model remains most resilient to performance drops when heads with the lowest importance scores are gradually removed. In contrast, removing the most important attention head first results in the greatest reduction in classification performance. A closer look at individual head importance scores across six attention layers reveals that important heads are scattered across layers, with no consistent layer-specific trends. In contrast to the image and language domains, the importance of individual attention heads varies considerably across tabular datasets with different schemas and feature spaces. The proposed importance score can improve efficiency and redundancy within transformer architectures. We make the source code for measuring the importance of individual attention heads publicly available.

</details>


### [327] [Circuit Condensation: Post-Training that Concentrates a Behavior's Causal Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27254)
*Sai Adith Senthil Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"电路压缩"（Circuit Condensation）方法，通过后训练优化使模型将行为集中在更小的因果图上，相比冻结电路发现，平均缩小8.1倍（最高316倍），并证明权重更新而非搜索本身是缩减的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性中通过电路（components and connections）解释模型行为时，冻结电路发现方法往往返回数百条边，使其难以检查、比较或彻底验证。

Method: 迭代过程：每轮剪除低归因边，并训练低秩适配器（low-rank adapter）通过保留的边匹配原始模型行为，仅当任务性能与通用能力保持时才保留剪除。同时系统性地测试电路子集、成对消融以揭示边间依赖。

Result: 在4种行为、8个模型上，压缩电路在32个设置中的30个优于最强冻结基线，平均小8.1倍、最大316倍。不加权重更新的重复搜索在29个设置中产生更大电路。间接宾语识别中，压缩仅24个头（17个有记录角色），对比冻结电路的61个头（36个无记录）。

Conclusion: 电路压缩能产生充分（sufficient）而非重构（reconstruction）的发布机制子电路，电路能追踪原始模型的下一词分布并预测其错误。

Abstract: One approach to mechanistic interpretability explains behavior through circuits: the components and connections that carry it. Frozen discovery often returns hundreds of edges, making them hard to inspect, compare, or verify exhaustively. We introduce Circuit Condensation, which post-trains models to concentrate behaviors into smaller causal graphs. Each round prunes low-attribution edges and trains a low-rank adapter to match the original through what remains, retaining the cut only if task performance and general capability survive. Across four behaviors and eight models, condensed circuits are smaller than the strongest frozen baseline in 30 of 32 settings, by $8.1\times$ on average and up to $316\times$. Repeating the search without weight updates produces larger circuits in 29 of 32 settings, showing that weight updates, rather than search alone, drive the reduction. Testing every subset of 19 circuits finds 11 that cannot be reduced and reveals removable edges in the rest. Pair ablations expose dependencies between edges, showing that their effects cannot be understood independently. On indirect object identification, condensation isolates 24 heads, 17 of them with documented roles, against 61 heads and 36 undocumented ones for the matched frozen circuit: a sufficient sub-circuit of the published mechanism rather than a reconstruction of it. The resulting circuit tracks the original model's next-token distribution and predicts its errors.

</details>


### [328] [Making Latent Evolution Explicit: Operator-Structured Transitions for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27259)
*Xiaoxiao Lu,Yunlong Dong,Jiahao Shi,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LEON（潜空间演化算子网络），将潜空间世界动作模型（WAM）中的转移实现从基于Transformer的标记交互范式，重构为受控Koopman生成器视角下的上下文调制算子传播与加性强迫机制，显著提升闭环性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜空间WAM多采用Transformer类预测器，其归纳偏置聚焦于token交互而非时间演化，导致转移实现这一架构选择未被充分研究。作者认为转移实现是与预测表示、预测-策略耦合相互独立的架构维度，值得单独审视。

Method: LEON在可学习的可观测量空间中建模潜演化：通过上下文调制的算子传播捕获共享的演化结构，同时保留加性强迫项作为补充路径。该方法以受控Koopman生成器理论为理论基础，将上下文相关的转移变异组织在共享演化算子结构周围。

Result: 在受控动力系统上验证了演化特异性归纳偏置及算子传播与强迫的互补作用。在两种不同的WAM公式（潜预测以不同方式集成到策略中）下，LEON均改善了闭环性能与鲁棒性，且在完全转移替换下依然有效。

Conclusion: 转移实现在潜空间WAM中是一个具有重要影响的架构选择，LEON证明了基于算子传播的演化建模优于以token交互为核心的Transformer范式。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies by predicting how task-relevant scene states may evolve under interaction. Recent WAMs increasingly perform such prediction in latent representation spaces, avoiding full appearance-level generation while preserving control-relevant information. Yet latent transitions are commonly realized with Transformer-based predictors whose inductive structure is centered on token interaction rather than temporal evolution. We study transition realization as an architectural choice distinct from predictive representation and prediction-policy coupling. We introduce the Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON), which models latent evolution in a learned observable space through context-modulated operator-based propagation and additive forcing. Grounded in the controlled Koopman generator view of evolution, LEON organizes context-dependent transition variation around a shared evolution-operator structure while retaining a complementary path for additive change. Controlled dynamical systems verify the resulting evolution-specific inductive bias and the complementary roles of operator propagation and forcing. Across two WAM formulations that integrate latent prediction into the policy differently, LEON improves closed-loop performance and robustness while remaining effective under full transition replacement. These results establish transition realization as a consequential architectural choice in latent WAMs.

</details>


### [329] [MM-Spectrum: Multimodal Multi-spectral Molecular Structural Elucidation with a Stable MoE Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27286)
*Hai-tao Yu,Nan Min,Zheng Fang,Hongyu Zhan,Yusen Tan,Yuhan Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-Spectrum，一个基于稀疏Mixture-of-Experts的多模态多光谱谱图到分子结构解析框架。针对直接拼接多光谱序列导致的性能退化问题，引入显式模态感知路由机制和共享/交互专家，在完整模态、双模态和缺失模态场景下均取得一致且显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多模态光谱测量中推断分子结构需要整合互补但高度异构的信号。然而，直接拼接多光谱序列会出现异常的性能退化，主要原因是模态间显著的异质性导致的多模态不平衡问题。这一缺陷促使研究者设计专门的框架来应对。

Method: MM-Spectrum采用稀疏Mixture-of-Experts架构，包含三个关键设计：(1) 显式模态感知路由机制，将光谱身份与token内容表示一起暴露给路由器；(2) 共享专家和交互专家，分别提取模态独有信息和跨模态协同信息；(3) 异构专家容量，以抑制噪声干扰并更好匹配多光谱不平衡下的信息特性。

Result: 在分子结构解析的全模态、双模态和缺失模态三种设置下，MM-Spectrum均取得一致且显著的性能提升，并通过消融实验和可解释性分析得到验证。

Conclusion: MM-Spectrum有效解决了多光谱模态不平衡导致的多模态集成性能退化问题，为多模态光谱到结构解析提供了新的解决方案，并在各种模态设置下展现出鲁棒性和优越性。

Abstract: Inferring molecular structures from multimodal spectroscopic measurements requires integrating complementary yet highly heterogeneous signals. However, the common paradigm of directly concatenating multispectral sequences can exhibit anomalous performance degradation, primarily due to pronounced heterogeneity and the resulting multimodal imbalance across modalities. As a remedy, we propose MM-Spectrum, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts framework tailored for multimodal multispectral spectra-to-structure elucidation. To better match the information characteristics under multispectral imbalance, MM-Spectrum introduces an explicit modality-aware routing mechanism that exposes spectral identity to the router in addition to token content representations. Moreover, it incorporates shared and interaction experts, together with heterogeneous expert capacities, to extract multispectral modality-unique and cross-modal synergistic information while suppressing noise-induced interference. Across full-modality, bimodal, and missing-modality settings on molecular structural elucidation, MM-Spectrum achieves consistent and substantial improvements, supported by ablation studies and interpretability analyses.

</details>


### [330] [QuantumBoostNet: A Hybrid Classical-Quantum Architecture for Enhanced Accuracy in Cardiac Ultrasound View Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27302)
*Mihai Udrescu-Milosav,Stefan-Alexandru Jura,Mihai Udrescu,Gerhard-Paul Diller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QuantumBoostNet，一种混合经典-量子架构，用于心脏超声（超声心动图）视图/角度识别任务。该模型结合经典骨干网络与双头结构（一个经典头、一个10量子比特参数化量子电路头），通过两阶段训练和混合参数自适应切换，在心脏超声视图识别任务上超越现有经典与混合模型，并展现出对噪声的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏超声视图的正确识别对心脏成像至关重要，直接影响解剖解释的准确性和临床错误率的降低。尽管计算机视觉已取得显著进展，但最先进的模型在标准基准上表现良好，在医疗影像这类高噪声数据上往往效果欠佳，因此需要更鲁棒、更精准的专用模型。

Method: 提出QuantumBoostNet混合架构：包含一个经典骨干网络，以及双头结构——一个经典头和一个由10量子比特参数化量子电路实现的量子头。训练采用两阶段策略，通过一个监控损失动态的混合参数实现两头的自适应转换，以平衡经典与量子特征提取的优势。

Result: 大量实验表明，尽管可模拟的量子比特数量有限，QuantumBoostNet在心脏超声视图识别任务上始终优于最先进的经典模型和混合经典-量子模型，相对最佳竞争者取得了改善。同时该模型在标准图像分类基准上也表现更优，并展现出对噪声的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持继续开发面向专业医疗影像应用的混合经典-量子模型，表明量子计算与经典深度学习的结合在噪声较大的医学成像任务中具有实际潜力。

Abstract: Accurate identification of the correct view or angle in cardiac ultrasound (echocardiogram) is a critical component of cardiologic imaging. This step is essential for precise anatomical interpretation, reliable measurement, and the reduction of clinical errors. Although computer vision has advanced significantly, most state-of-the-art models perform well on standard benchmarks but often yield suboptimal results in specialized medical imaging tasks due to the high level of noise present in the data. QuantumBoostNet, a hybrid classical-quantum architecture, is introduced to address these challenges. This model integrates a classical backbone with two heads: one classical and one quantum, with the quantum head implemented as a parametrized 10-qubit quantum circuit. Training occurs in two stages, with an adaptive transition between heads governed by a mixing parameter that monitors loss dynamics. Extensive experiments indicate that, despite the limited number of qubits that can be simulated, QuantumBoostNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art classical and hybrid classical-quantum models in cardiac ultrasound view identification, achieving a relative improvement over the best competitor. QuantumBoostNet also demonstrates superior performance on established image classification benchmarks and exhibits robustness to noise. These findings support the continued development of hybrid classical-quantum models for specialized medical imaging applications.

</details>


### [331] [Beyond Parallel Blindness: Information Floors and Model Gaps in Block Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27339)
*Xinwei Qiang,Xiang Fang,Chang Chen,Yue Guan,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过引入"信息下限"（information floor）概念，将块草稿者（block drafter）的拒绝率分解为两部分：由于缺少块内路径信息造成的拒绝（信息下限）和由于对可观测信息建模不完善造成的拒绝（模型差距）。通过在四个域、四个开放权重目标和前沿API目标上的实验，发现：全并行设置下信息下限高达0.286（Qwen3-4B），一个已实现token可消除86%-100%的该下限，当前草稿者仍远高于其信息下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块草稿者一次前向传播提出多个token，但它们的拒绝混合了两种不同损失：缺少块内路径信息（因为早期目标token尚未生成）和对可观测信息建模不完善。仅靠接受长度无法区分这两种损失，需要分解以分别评估短程条件化的价值和草稿者质量。

Method: 提出"信息下限"概念，即指定条件顺序下拒绝率的最小期望值；拒绝率高于该下限的部分定义为"模型差距"。通过目标（target）rollouts在四个域、四个开放权重目标模型和一个前沿API目标模型上估计这两个指标。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）全并行时Qwen3-4B最终槽位的信息下限达0.286，即使最优草稿者每次槽位接受率也只有71%；（2）一个已实现token可消除86%-100%的信息下限，该局部性也由独立的互信息分析验证；（3）当前草稿者远高于其下限：DFlash最终槽位拒绝的43%-64%来自模型差距，DSpark预言机条件化拒绝的85%-92%来自模型差距。

Conclusion: 该分解方法成功将短程条件化的价值与草稿者质量分离，揭示了块草稿中的拒绝主要由模型差距主导，而非信息下限，为改进块草稿策略提供了清晰的方向。

Abstract: Block drafters propose several tokens in one forward pass, before earlier target tokens are realised. Their rejection mixes two losses: missing within-block path information and imperfect modelling of observable information. Accepted length cannot distinguish them. We separate the two with an information floor, the minimum expected rejection at a specified conditioning order; rejection above this floor is the model gap. Estimating both from target rollouts across four domains, four open-weight targets, and a frontier API target yields three findings. First, the all-parallel floor reaches $0.286$ at the final slot on Qwen3-4B, limiting even the best proposal to $71\%$ per-slot acceptance. Second, one realised token removes $86$--$100\%$ of this floor, a locality also recovered by an independent mutual-information analysis. Third, current drafters remain far above their floors: the final-slot model gap accounts for $43$--$64\%$ of DFlash rejection and $85$--$92\%$ of DSpark's oracle-conditioned rejection. These findings separate the value of short-range conditioning from proposal quality.

</details>


### [332] [Understanding Evolution Strategies for LLM Reasoning: Broader Reasoning Coverage than GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27351)
*Yunpeng Ba,Zhi Zheng,Yue Xie,Jiaqing Li,Xialiang Tong,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan,Zhichao Lu,Xuyang Wu,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了进化策略（ES）作为LLM推理后训练范式的优化行为，发现ES相比GRPO能产生更广泛的推理覆盖，提升Pass@K性能，且其性能增益仅来自稀疏的大幅度参数更新子集，不会导致灾难性遗忘，并将ES定位为一种独特而非逊色的推理后训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ES近年来被提出作为一种内存高效的LLM推理后训练范式，但其优化行为尚未被充分研究，难以明确其相较于主流范式（如GRPO）的优势范围。

Method: 1) 系统研究ES的动态与机制；2) 理论上证明验证器投影的Jensen-Shannon多样性有助于提升Pass@K；3) 提出顺序GRPO-ES训练策略，结合两者优势；4) 分析参数漂移与功能稀疏性的关系；5) 研究超参数（如种群规模）对ES效果的影响。

Result: 1) ES在Pass@K上优于GRPO，而GRPO存在熵坍缩问题；2) ES在提升Pass@1的同时可获得更高的Pass@K；3) 顺序GRPO-ES策略有效结合两者优势；4) ES的性能增益仅来自稀疏的大幅度更新子集，参数大幅移动不意味着广泛功能变化，也不必然导致灾难性遗忘；5) 更大的LLM需要更小的ES种群规模。

Conclusion: ES应被视为一种独特的推理后训练范式，而非GRPO的低效内存替代品，其优势在于更广的推理覆盖和更高的Pass@K，且具有功能稀疏性和对遗忘的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) have recently emerged as a memory-efficient post-training paradigm for LLM reasoning. However, the optimization behavior of ES remains understudied, making it hard to define its advantage scope compared to mainstream post-training paradigms (e.g., Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)). By systematically investigating ES dynamics and mechanisms, this paper first identifies a performance advantage of ES over GRPO, theoretically and empirically showing that ES can lead to broader reasoning coverage, thereby better exploiting the reasoning capabilities of pretrained LLMs. Theoretically, we show that verifier-projected Jensen-Shannon diversity across the ES population is helpful to higher Pass@K performances. Empirically, unlike GRPO, which exhibits entropy collapse, ES improves Pass@1 while attaining higher Pass@K than GRPO. We further develop a sequential GRPO-ES training strategy that combines GRPO's strength in Pass@1 with ES's gains in Pass@K. Second, we find that despite substantial whole-model parameter drift, the task-performance gains of ES are only contributed to a sparse subset of larger-magnitude updates. This functional sparsity suggests that large parameter movement need not imply widespread functional change, and held-out evaluations further show that it does not necessarily lead to catastrophic forgetting. Finally, we study how hyperparameter design affects the effectiveness of ES, demonstrating that ES requires a smaller population size in a larger LLM. These findings position ES as a distinct reasoning post-training paradigm rather than a less effective, memory-efficient alternative to GRPO.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [333] [Revision-Aware Success Prediction from Multi-Attempt Programming Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26169)
*Md Faizul Ibne Amin,Yutaka Watanobe,Daniel M. Muepu,Kenta Nanaumi,Haruto Suzuki,Md. Shahajada Mia,Md Mostafizer Rahman*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究在统一框架下系统地比较了三种编程提交结果预测任务（当前提交是否被接受、下一次提交是否被接受、三次提交内是否达到接受）以及三种输入模式（仅当前、成对、多步），使用ML、DL和Transformer预训练模型（LinearSVM、XGBoost、BiGRU、BiLSTM、GraphCodeBERT、CodeT5+）进行对比。结果显示仅当前输入模式最可靠，ML模型整体最强最稳定，Task 2最难，复杂模型仅在特定场景中有价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程结果预测在教育数据驱动中至关重要，但由于错误状态的异质性、短期修订行为和未来时间窗口的不均匀可用性，预测提交成功非常困难。现有研究缺乏对不同预测任务和输入模式进行统一、系统比较的工作。

Method: 将三个预测任务统一形式化（当前尝试接受与否、下一尝试接受与否、三次尝试恢复窗口内是否接受），在三种输入模式（仅当前、成对、多步）下评估六种模型：线性SVM、XGBoost（ML）、BiGRU、BiLSTM（DL）、GraphCodeBERT、CodeT5+（PTM），并进行灵敏度分析以控制未来时间窗口。

Result: 仅当前输入模式最可靠，成对和多步历史无一致增益；ML模型整体最强最稳定，尤其在Task 1和3；Task 2对所有模型族最难。在Task 3仅当前模式下，XGBoost达到AP/PR-AUC 99.09%和MCC 0.6325，GraphCodeBERT和CodeT5+分别达F1 80.00%和73.68%。

Conclusion: ML模型在编程成功预测中保持高效，复杂模型仅在特定设置中有价值。该研究为编程教育的提交感知分析提供了系统化比较和稳健的建模指导，可支持在线评测平台和自适应编程支持系统中的及时干预。

Abstract: Programming outcome prediction plays a central role in data-driven programming education, supporting learner modeling, timely intervention, and adaptive assistance. Yet predicting submission success is difficult due to heterogeneous error states, short-term revisions, and uneven future-horizon availability in programming trajectories. This study examines three prediction tasks under a unified formulation: whether the current attempt is accepted (Task~1), whether the next attempt is accepted (Task~2), and whether acceptance is reached within a three-attempt recovery window (Task~3). Each task is evaluated across current-only, pairwise, and multi-step input regimes using ML, DL, and transformer-based pretrained models (PTM), represented by LinearSVM, XGBoost, BiGRU, BiLSTM, GraphCodeBERT, and CodeT5+. Results show a consistent pattern: the current-only regime is the most reliable, while pairwise and multi-step history provide no consistent gain. ML models are the strongest and most stable overall, particularly in Tasks~1 and~3, and Task~2 is the hardest across all model families. DL and PTMs perform well on Task~3 but are more task-dependent. In the Task~3 current-only setting, XGBoost achieves AP/PR-AUC of 99.09% and MCC of 0.6325, while GraphCodeBERT and CodeT5+ reach F1 scores of 80.00% and 73.68%, respectively. A sensitivity analysis confirms that Task~3 conclusions hold most robustly for ML models under stricter future-horizon control. Across all settings, ML models remain highly effective for programming success prediction, while complex models offer value in specific settings. This work provides a systematic comparison across predictive formulations and offers robust modeling guidance for submission-aware analytics in programming education, where near-future success prediction can inform timely intervention in online judge platforms and adaptive programming support systems.

</details>


### [334] [Mitigating Fabrication in Multi-Stage LLM Pipelines for Hiring: An Empirical Evaluation of Prompt Guardrails and Human-in-the-Loop Checkpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26171)
*Hiroko Takano*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 多阶段LLM招聘流水线（简历优化、面试题生成、答案反馈）可能虚构证书、夸大资质、编造经历。本文通过180次受控实验评估了提示护栏和人类介入(HITL)检查点两种缓解措施。基线在96.7%的输出中产生至少一条无依据声明；提示护栏将发现密度降低86%但仍有一半输出包含虚构；人类检查点消除了身份虚构、将条目级虚构从96.7%降至75.0%，但两种措施均未降低声明保留率（>99%）。结果表明应结合护栏与人工检查点构建分层架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段LLM招聘流水线在简历改进、面试题生成和答案反馈等环节可能产生虚假信息，损害招聘流程的公平性和可靠性。现有研究缺乏对这类流水线中虚构/伪造问题的系统量化评估，以及不同缓解策略（提示级与人类介入级）效果的对比研究。

Method: 在受控实验中，使用10个合成简历×2个职位描述×3次重复×3种条件（基线C1、提示护栏C2、人类检查点C3），共180次运行。同时设计了JD嵌入陷阱需求以测试模型对"诱饵"要求的捕获率，并进行探索性多专业跨度分析和更新代模型的补充运行。

Result: 基线在96.7%输出中产生虚构（平均6.80条/输出）；提示护栏将发现密度降低86%（至0.92/输出）但50%输出仍有虚构；人类检查点消除了所有身份虚构、将条目级虚构从96.7%降至75.0%（p=.022）、JD陷阱捕获率从47%降至2%。审查者捕获了所有明显虚构，但对微妙资格降级和合理新声明的移除率仅54.5%。两种干预下声明保留率均超99%。专业跨度越大信息污染越严重。更新代模型基线虚构率仍达90.0%。

Conclusion: 提示级缓解本身不足以解决LLM招聘流水线的虚构问题；建议采用分层架构，将成本低廉的提示护栏（消除非提示添加和资质膨胀）与提供最严重故障（身份虚构和JD诱饵声明）近乎确定性保障的人类检查点相结合。模型能力进步本身不能解决该问题。

Abstract: Multi-stage LLM hiring pipelines (resume improvement, interview question generation, answer feedback) can fabricate credentials, inflate qualifiers, and invent experience. We evaluate two mitigations, prompt guardrails and human-in-the-loop (HITL) checkpoints, against a fully automated baseline. In a controlled experiment (10 synthetic resumes x 2 job descriptions x 3 repetitions x 3 conditions; 180 runs), the baseline (C1) produced at least one unsupported claim in 96.7% of outputs (mean 6.80 findings/output). Prompt guardrails (C2) reduced finding density by 86% (6.80 to 0.92/output), but 50.0% of outputs still contained a fabrication, showing prompt-level mitigation alone is insufficient. A human checkpoint after resume improvement (C3) eliminated all identity fabrications, reduced finding density by 59% (6.88 to 2.82/output), reduced item-level fabrication from 96.7% to 75.0% (p=.022), and cut capture of JD-embedded trap requirements from 47% to 2% (vs. 5% under the guardrail). An exploratory analysis of multi-specialty resumes shows contamination rising monotonically with domain distance between specialties, suggesting career changers are especially exposed. The reviewer in this study caught all flagrant fabrications, but subtle qualifier drops and plausible new claims survived review roughly half the time (54.5% removal). Neither mitigation degraded the deliverable: claim retention exceeded 99% under both. The interventions are complementary: the guardrail eliminates unprompted additions and qualifier inflation cheaply, while the checkpoint gives near-categorical guarantees against the most severe failures, invented identities and JD-baited claims. These results support a layered architecture combining guardrails with a human checkpoint. A supplementary run with a newer-generation model (90.0% baseline fabrication rate) suggests the problem is not resolved by model progress alone.

</details>


### [335] [ClassVision: AI-Powered Classroom Attendance System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26173)
*Ankit Kumar Aggarwal,Veerabhadra Rao Marellapudi,Ovadia Sutton,Youshan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出并构建了ClassVision课堂考勤系统，利用人脸检测和人脸识别技术实现课堂考勤自动化。研究发现RetinaFace是最佳的人脸检测模型，结合Face Recognition进行验证，在50x50像素裁剪嵌入下取得了最佳效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的纸质或在线考勤方式耗时且依赖人工，效率低下。为了应对传统人工考勤流程中的挑战，本研究探索利用人脸检测和人脸识别技术自动化考勤过程，特别是在教育环境中。

Method: 构建了ClassVision课程考勤系统，该系统具有人机交互（HCI）和用户友好的Web界面，利用实时图像处理来识别和识别教室中的学生并自动记录出勤。通过对比实验确定了RetinaFace作为最佳人脸检测模型，并与Face Recognition结合进行人脸验证。

Result: RetinaFace与Face Recognition结合在50x50像素的裁剪嵌入下提供了最有前景的结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于人脸检测和识别的自动化考勤系统能够有效替代传统人工考勤方式，RetinaFace与Face Recognition的组合是实现该系统的优选方案。

Abstract: Students and working professionals have to go through the attendance process every day. Traditional methods of marking attendance using pen and paper or online platforms are human-intensive and time-consuming. To address the challenges in manual attendance processes, this research explores the use of face detection (FD) and face recognition (FR) technology to automate the attendance process, particularly in educational settings, and build a ClassVision course attendance system. We also propose an automated attendance system featuring a human-computer interaction (HCI) and user-friendly web interface that utilizes real-time image processing to identify and recognize students in classrooms and automatically record their attendance. We identified RetinaFace as the best face detection model, and when combined with Face Recognition for verification, it provided the most promising results with a cropped embedding of 50x50 pixels.

</details>


### [336] [How Does Science Education Research Respond to Sociopolitical Change? A BERTopic Analysis of Korean Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26675)
*Jibeom Seo,Junghyo Jo,Sonya N. Martin,Taejin Byun*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究使用BERTopic主题建模方法分析了2008至2025年韩国科学教育研究文献，考察其与社会政治因素（如课程改革、政府政策、人口下降）的关联。研究发现三类主题：社会政治类、学科特定类和学生相关类。社会政治类主题（如教师专业发展、资优生教育、STEAM教育）与外部政策条件紧密相关，而学科特定类和学生相关类主题则表现出更强的学科根基，较少受外部变化影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往科学教育研究主要关注识别研究主题及其趋势，但很少关注研究生产所处的外部条件（如政策、课程改革、社会变化）如何影响研究议程。本文旨在填补这一空白，探讨研究领域与外部社会政治条件之间的关系。

Method: 采用BERTopic嵌入式主题建模技术，对2008至2025年韩国科学教育研究文献进行分析，识别主要主题及时序趋势，并将主题与选定的社会政治因素（集中式课程修订、政府教育举措、人口下降等）进行关联分析。

Result: 研究发现三类主题群组：社会政治类、学科特定类和学生相关类。其中，科学教师专业性与课程实施、资优生科学教育、STEAM教育等社会政治类主题与外部政策条件（如政府政策举措、科学资优教育入学率下降）关联最强；而数字化科学教育则不然。学科特定类和学生相关类主题与所考察的外部指标没有可比的变动或关联，反映了更强的学科基础。

Conclusion: 主题对政策与实践的锚定程度，或对学术学科的锚定程度，决定了其追踪外部变化的紧密程度。这有助于解释为何某些研究议程随国家背景变动而其他保持不变，以及为何同一主题在不同国家可能呈现不同的发展轨迹。

Abstract: Research fields do not evolve in isolation: their questions and priorities shift with policy, curriculum reform, and broader social change. Analyzing published literature can reveal not only how a field matures but also how it responds to these conditions. Prior work in science education has focused on identifying research topics and their trends, but paid less attention to the external conditions in which research is produced. We examine Korean science education research from 2008 to 2025, a case in which centralized curriculum revision, government education initiatives, and demographic decline are prominent. Using BERTopic, an embedding-based topic modeling technique, we identify major topics and temporal trends, and analyze their associations with selected sociopolitical factors. We interpret each topic and distinguish three groups: sociopolitical, subject-specific, and student-related topics. Within the first group, science teacher professionalism and curriculum implementation, science education for gifted students, and STEAM education show the strongest associations with sociopolitical conditions, such as government policy initiatives and declining enrollment in science-gifted education, whereas digital-based science education does not. The subject-specific and student-related groups, by contrast, show no comparable movement and are not linked to the external indicators we examine; this pattern is interpreted as reflecting stronger disciplinary grounding. Taken together, these patterns suggest that a topic's anchoring to policy and practice or to academic disciplines shapes how closely it tracks external change. This helps explain why some research agendas move with their national context while others hold steady, and why the same topic may develop differently across countries.

</details>


### [337] [Reclaiming Epistemic Agency: A Critical Framework for Human-Generative AI Co-Agency in Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26937)
*Biranchi Poudyal*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对生成式人工智能（GenAI）在教育中的应用进行了概念性评估，指出GenAI不仅是一种教学工具，更重新分配了教师、学生与机器之间的认识论权威。论文评估了多种理论范式后发现它们均未解决权力分配、数据所有权和人类问责制等关键问题，由此提出"生态共能动框架"（Ecological Co-Agency Framework）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将GenAI仅视为中立的教育工具，忽视了其在课堂中重新分配认识论权威（从教师到学生再到机器）这一更深层次的转变。作者旨在揭示并填补这一理论空白。

Method: 采用概念性评估方法，综合运用多重理论范式（分布式能动性、自我决定理论、Society 5.0、TPACK与SAMR技术整合范式），系统分析这些理论在处理GenAI引发的教育能动性再分配问题上的适用性与不足，并据此提出新的理论框架。

Result: 评估发现，所有被检视的理论范式虽与AI中介教育中的能动性相关，但均未充分解决教育中权力公平分配、用于中介互动的数据所有权、以及人对中介互动问责制方面的持续不平等问题。由此提出生态共能动框架，将能动性定义为关系性、规范性与教学性过程的集合，并以人类对认识论主张的问责承诺为条件。

Conclusion: GenAI带来的不仅是工具层面的变化，更是认识论权力结构的根本性重构。需要新的理论框架来确保人类在教育中对知识主张保持问责，生态共能动框架为此提供了初步的理论方向。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) has been primarily framed as an impartial educational tool. However, this framing overlooks an even larger shift: the reassignment of epistemological authority from teachers to students to machines. This paper presents a conceptual evaluation of the extent to which GenAI redistributes students' and teachers' ability to act in classrooms to produce knowledge, validate each other's claims, and create evidence of student learning while collaborating with and competing against humans. This evaluation draws on various theoretical paradigms, including Distributed Agency, Self-Determination Theory, Society 5.0, and Technology Integration Paradigms, including TPACK and SAMR. While all of the theoretical paradigms evaluated are relevant to the role of agency within education mediated by AI, none of them address the ongoing disparity regarding equitable distribution of power, ownership of the data used to mediate interaction, and accountability in relation to human-mediated interactions. As such, this paper introduces the Ecological Co-Agency Framework, which defines agency in terms of relational, regulatory, and pedagogical processes, conditioned by a defined commitment to human accountability for epistemological claims.

</details>


### [338] [Animarium: an open, reproducible pipeline for synthetic populations of Italian cities, from ISTAT sources to open data (Tech Report v1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27111)
*Mirko Degli Esposti*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个完全基于公开聚合数据生成意大利11个城镇（1,814,317人）合成人口的方法论与平台。管线采用四环结构（最大熵联合属性模型、整向量捐赠、空间定位、家庭约束），全程确定性可复现（字节级一致，33分钟生成），并以开放数据格式发布，配有Animarium可浏览查看器，同时配套LLM驱动的叙事层用于仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建大规模、真实、隐私合规的合成人口数据。现有方法或依赖受控的个体级原始数据，或缺乏全流程的可追溯性与可复现性。作者希望仅从公开发布的聚合数据出发，生成可用于社会仿真、流行病学建模和AI驱动模拟的高保真合成人群，同时确保数据发布的法律与伦理合规性。

Method: 采用"四环"（four rings）体系架构：第一环用最大熵模型联合建模多达9个人口学属性；第二环从调查受访者中整体捐赠23个态度与健康变量；第三环将人口定位到普查区、单岁年龄和地址（坐标在普查区内随机化）；第四环按普查区的家庭规模分布约束住户结构。所有数据源均来自公开渠道（ISTAT普查、注册表、CIVIC地址库、开放数据门户等），并在注册表中记录许可证、指纹与声明用途。管线完全确定性，由标记提交重现；下游层（详细头衔、职业、姓名、传记）均为显式声明的派生数据。提供Animarium无依赖Web查看器和结构化开放数据集。

Result: 从纯公开聚合数据成功生成11个城镇约181万人口的合成人群，分布在887,937户中。管线在单台工作站上33分钟内完成全部城镇的位对位（bit-for-bit）复现，验证了完全确定性。所有数据在强制隐私制度下（无姓名、无地址、坐标在普查区内随机化）以开放数据集形式发布，并提供可浏览、可引用URL的Web查看器。同时实现了LLM驱动的叙事层，将记录渲染为人设用于仿真。

Conclusion: 作者展示了仅依赖公开聚合数据构建大规模、可复现、隐私合规的合成人口是可行的，确立了数据溯源（来源认证）与可复现性（字节级确定性）作为合成数据平台的核心原则。验证（validation）被明确排除在本文范围之外，暗示该平台定位为生成与发布基础设施，后续将结合配套实验的仿真可控性进行应用验证。

Abstract: Synthetic populations of eleven Italian municipalities (1,814,317 individuals in 887,937 households) generated from published aggregates alone: ISTAT census and register tables, census-section counts, the national civic-address register, public-use survey microdata, and six municipal open-data portals, every source certified in a registry with licence, fingerprint and declared affordances. Four rings give every attribute a declared place: a maximum-entropy joint model of up to nine demographic attributes; whole-vector donation of twenty-three attitudinal and health variables from survey respondents; placement to census section, single year of age and address; and households constrained by the census size distribution per section. Every downstream layer (detailed titles, work, names, biographies) is a declared derivation adding no information. The pipeline is deterministic to the byte: regenerating all eleven municipalities from the tagged commit reproduces every file of every ring bit for bit, in 33 minutes on one workstation. Populations are released in a public regime enforced in the data (no names, no addresses, coordinates randomised within census section), browsable in Animarium, a dependency-free web viewer where every number carries its comparison and every view is a citable URL, and downloadable as an open dataset. The report documents the architecture, the sources and their certification, the reproducibility and quality measurements at the release tag, the viewer, and the narrative layer that renders records into personas for LLM-driven simulation, with the platform's controllability demonstrated in companion experiments, and validation explicitly out of scope.

</details>


### [339] [Assessing Company Contributions to Societal Resilience: Extending the Societal Capacity Assessment Framework to Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27238)
*Catherine Simons,Alexander K. Saeri,Peter Slattery,Neil Thompson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的基于指标的"社会能力评估框架"(SCAF)，从评估AI部署的社会背景下测量社会韧性，转变为评估公司Agentic AI部署决策本身如何贡献于社会韧性，并在微软公开文档上进行结构化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署AI代理的公司是制度性行动者，积极塑造社会抵御和治理Agentic AI后果的能力。为增强社会韧性，需要将测量框架从"部署决策的背景"倒转为"评估公司部署决策本身的贡献"，从而运营化这一目标。

Method: 分两步：1) 概念步骤——设计一套指标，界定SCAF的脆弱性、应对能力和适应能力在评估公司Agentic AI部署决策时的含义；2) 测量步骤——将该框架应用于对微软公开文档的结构化评估。

Result: 成功将SCAF框架倒转应用，从衡量社会韧性作为部署决策的背景，转变为衡量公司部署决策对社会韧性的贡献，并通过微软文档案例验证了框架的可行性。

Conclusion: 公司可以通过设计并推广更安全的AI代理实现来构建社会韧性，本文提供的SCAF倒转框架为运营化这一目标提供了可操作的评估工具。

Abstract: Companies that deploy AI agents and make them available to others are creating the sociotechnical circumstances under which this technology integrates into existing social and economic structures. AI-deploying companies are institutional actors that actively shape society's capacity to withstand and govern the consequences of agentic AI. In view of these societal impacts, companies can build societal resilience by designing and promoting safer implementations of AI agents. To operationalize this goal, this paper adapts the indicator-based Societal Capacity Assessment Framework (SCAF) to measure how a company's deployment decisions contribute to societal resilience, inverting its original measurement of societal resilience as a backdrop for deployment decisions (Gandhi et al., 2025). Our procedure has two steps: a conceptual step in which we design a suite of indicators that define what SCAF's vulnerability, coping, and adaptive capacities mean when assessing a company's agentic AI deployment decisions; and a measurement step in which we apply this framework in a structured assessment of public-facing Microsoft documents.

</details>


<div id='econ.EM'></div>

# econ.EM [[Back]](#toc)

### [340] [Inference for High-Dimensional Network Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26522)
*Yuya Sasaki,Baoning Zheng*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对网络依赖的高维随机向量的推断方法，基于图距离的泛函依赖度量来刻画依赖结构，建立了最大范数下的高斯逼近理论，并提出高维网络HAC协方差估计器用于同时推断。模拟实验验证了有限样本性能，并将方法应用于研究网络同质性指数下的溢出效应异质性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高维统计推断方法假设数据独立或具有简单依赖结构，但现实中的网络数据具有复杂的依赖关系，且网络邻域随规模增长。现有方法难以同时处理高维性与网络依赖性，缺乏在维度随网络规模增长条件下的高斯逼近理论及可行的推断程序。

Method: 1) 提出基于图距离的泛函依赖度量来刻画网络依赖结构；2) 在有限矩和次Weibull条件下建立最大范数的高斯逼近理论，给出了维度随网络规模增长的显式条件；3) 提出高维网络HAC协方差估计器并建立收敛性质；4) 构建条件溢出效应函数的置信带进行实际推断。

Result: 模拟研究表明所提方法具有良好有限样本性能；应用于网络同质性指数下的溢出效应分析时，揭示了传统低维推断方法所掩盖的异质性和局部显著性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地将高维统计推断推广到网络依赖数据场景，为网络数据的高维同时推断提供了理论保障和可行工具，并在实际应用中发现了更有价值的异质性模式。

Abstract: We develop a novel method of inference for network-dependent high-dimensional random vectors. Dependence is characterized via a functional dependence measure based on graph distance, allowing the approximation theory to capture the interaction between the decay of dependence and the growth of network neighborhoods. We establish Gaussian approximation results for the maximum norm under finite-moment and sub-Weibull conditions, providing explicit conditions under which the dimension may increase with the network size. We also propose a high-dimensional network HAC covariance estimator and establish its convergence properties, yielding a feasible procedure for simultaneous inference. Simulation studies demonstrate favorable finite-sample performance of the proposed method. We apply the procedure to study how spillover effects vary with an index of network homophily by constructing confidence bands for the conditional spillover-effect function. The application reveals heterogeneity and local significance that would be obscured by conventional low-dimensional inference.

</details>


### [341] [DIRECT: Decomposing Audience Preference and Creative Effect in Visual Content Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26584)
*Yizhi Liu,Balaji Padmanabhan,Siva Viswanathan*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文指出视觉内容分析中常见的池化系数估计会混淆"受众偏好"和"创意效应"两种截然不同的模式，甚至可能给出相反的创作建议。作者提出DIRECT框架，结合面板因果推断与双机器学习，将两者分离，并在23万条Instagram美妆赞助帖中验证：11个视觉属性中有4个两种效应方向相反，池化系数在2个属性上推荐了错误的创作方向，导致31%的参与度增益损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献用跨创作者池化的系数来回答"哪种视觉选择让帖子表现更好"，但该系数混合了两种可能方向相反的效应：受众偏好（创作者风格吸引不同构成的受众）和创意效应（受众对创作者偏离其惯常风格的反应）。当受众偏好足够大时，会逆转创意方向所需的信号，导致平台给出的创作建议可能是错误的。

Method: 提出DIRECT（Decomposed Identification of Response Effects via Causal Tools）框架：1）利用面板数据结构，采用Mundlak的组间-组内分解将受众偏好与创意效应分离；2）结合双机器学习处理高维的潜在视觉-语言处理变量（从CLIP提取的视觉风格轴），这些变量在创作者内部共变；3）提供三个可迁移的诊断工具用于审计池化估计是否支持其所服务的决策。

Result: 在232,088条Instagram美妆赞助帖、1,527位创作者和11个CLIP视觉风格轴上验证：4/11的属性上受众偏好与创意效应方向相反；2/11的属性上池化系数本身推荐了错误的创作方向（如肤色属性上，池化系数偏好更浅肤色，而创意效应表明创作者受众对深于基线的肤色参与度更高）。在留出创作者上，基于池化系数开处方会损失31%的可实现参与度增益。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了三个方面：1）诊断了视觉内容分析中的估计量错配问题；2）提出了能从观测面板数据中恢复决策相关估计量的框架；3）提供了三个可移植的诊断工具用于审计池化估计是否支撑其服务的决策。研究揭示了平台创意推荐系统可能因估计量错配而给出系统性错误建议的隐患。

Abstract: Which visual choices make a post perform better? A growing literature answers this question with pooled coefficients estimated across many creators, which platforms translate into creative recommendations. We show that these coefficients blend two distinct patterns that can point in opposite directions for the same attribute. The first, audience preference, arises because creators who favor a style attract differently composed audiences, so their posts perform differently because of who is watching, not what any single post does. The second, creative effect, captures how a creator's audience responds when she departs from her usual look. Pooled estimation averages the two, and audience preference can be large enough to reverse the signal that creative direction requires. We propose DIRECT (Decomposed Identification of Response Effects via Causal Tools), a panel-based causal-inference framework that separates them, combining the Mundlak between-within decomposition with double machine learning over latent vision-language treatments that co-vary within a creator. We apply it to 232,088 sponsored Instagram beauty posts across 1,527 creators and 11 CLIP-derived visual style axes. The two carry opposite signs on 4 of 11 attributes, and on 2 of 11 the pooled coefficient itself recommends the wrong creative direction: on skin tone, it favors lighter representations while the creative effect points the other way, since a creator's audience engages more with tones darker than her baseline. On held-out creators, prescribing from the pooled coefficient forgoes 31% of the achievable engagement gain. We contribute a diagnosis of estimand mismatch in visual content analytics, a framework that recovers the decision-relevant estimand from observational panel data, and three portable diagnostics for auditing whether pooled estimates support the decisions they inform.

</details>


### [342] [Nonparametric Identification of Two-Way Unobserved Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27155)
*Hugo Freeman,Dennis Kristensen*

Main category: econ.EM

TL;DR: 本文研究非参数面板回归中双向不可观测异质性的识别问题，通过奇异值分解（SVD）构造潜在类型的单射代理变量。证明了观测等价简化与全体左奇异特征函数集合的单射性等价，且在一个局部单射条件下，有限个前导特征函数构成的向量对充分大的R是单射的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非参数面板回归模型G_it = g(α_i, γ_t) + ε_it中，识别潜在类型α和γ的关键在于构造可识别且单射的代理变量。已有方法通常需要全局雅可比条件的强假设，本文希望寻找更弱的充分条件来保证单射性。

Method: 利用乘积域Ω_α×Ω_γ上二元回归函数g(α,γ)的奇异值分解，将左奇异函数{u_r}作为α的代理变量。通过"观测等价简化"（两个α若导致相同条件响应则视为同一）来实现单射性构造。证明分两步：(1)该简化等价于全体左奇异特征函数集合的单射性；(2)在局部单射条件下，有限集合U_R=(u_1^T,...,u_R^T)^T对充分大的R是单射的。证明采用局部一阶条件加拓扑紧致性论证，绕过了全局雅可比条件。

Result: 两个主要结论：(1)观测等价简化与无限集合{u_r}_{r≥1}的单射性完全等价，无需额外条件；(2)在单一局部单射条件下，有限前导特征函数集合U_R对充分大的R即为单射。

Conclusion: 通过奇异值分解为双向不可观测异质性提供了可识别的代理变量，将全局单射问题简化为局部一阶条件加上拓扑紧致性论证，显著放宽了识别所需的假设条件。

Abstract: We study identification of two-way unobserved heterogeneity in the nonparametric panel regression $G_{it}=g(α_i,γ_t)+\varepsilon_{it}$, where identification of the latent types reduces to constructing identified, \emph{injective} proxies for them. To this end we consider the singular value decomposition (SVD) of the bivariate regression function $g(α,γ)$ on a product domain $Ω_α\timesΩ_γ$, whose left singular functions $\{u_r\}$ serve as proxies for the unobserved heterogeneity parameter $α$. The arguments are symmetric for $\{v_r\}$ vis-à-vis $γ$. We work under an \emph{observational-equivalence simplification}: two values of $α$ that induce the same conditional response $g(α,\cdot)$ are identified, so that the response map $α\mapsto g(α,\cdot)$ is injective by construction. We show two things. First, this reduction is \emph{equivalent} to injectivity of the full collection of left singular eigenfunctions, so no further condition is needed over the infinite collection $\{u_r\}_{r\ge1}$. Second, under a single additional \emph{local injectivity} condition, a finite collection of leading eigenfunctions $U_R=(u_1^{\top},\dots,u_R^{\top})^{\top}$ is injective for all sufficiently large $R$. The proof reduces a global univalence question to a local first-order condition plus a topological compactness argument, bypassing the global Jacobian conditions usually required.

</details>


<div id='q-fin.RM'></div>

# q-fin.RM [[Back]](#toc)

### [343] [DTD-VAE: Disentangled Temporal Dependencies VAE for Credit Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26473)
*Xiaobo Guo,Lu-an Dong,Yanbo Wang,Peng Zhang,Cai Zhi,Youru Li*

Main category: q-fin.RM

TL;DR: 本文提出DTD-VAE（解耦时间依赖变分自编码器），用于零售银行客户信用风险评估。该模型通过自回归时间依赖学习机制捕捉潜在变量间的时间依赖，并利用元素级门控机制对专家模型各维度分配独立权重，实现信用风险相关特征与客户偏好特征的精细化解耦。在六个真实数据集上，模型相比现有方法在ROC-AUC上提升3.2%-4.86%，在准确率比率上提升6.41%-9.71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信用评估方法难以捕捉复杂的时间依赖关系，且无法区分反映信用风险的时间模式与反映一般客户行为或偏好的模式，导致风险预测效果欠佳。

Method: DTD-VAE包含两个核心模块：1) 特征推断模块采用自回归时间依赖学习机制，捕捉潜在变量间的时间依赖关系；2) 特征生成模块利用元素级门控机制，为专家模型的每个维度分配独立权重，实现潜在变量的细粒度解耦，特别聚焦信用风险相关特征。

Result: 在六个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，DTD-VAE在ROC-AUC上取得3.2%-4.86%的提升，在Accuracy Ratio上取得6.41%-9.71%的提升，持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: DTD-VAE通过时间依赖解耦和细粒度特征分离，有效提升了零售银行信用风险评估的准确性，为信用风险预测提供了更优的建模方案。

Abstract: Evaluating customer creditworthiness is crucial for retail banking operations, as it impacts marketing strategies, customer relationship management, and credit risk control. Traditional methods often struggle to capture complex temporal dependencies and extract pertinent information from customer data, crucial for accurate risk assessment. Specifically, they fail to differentiate between temporal patterns indicative of credit risk and those reflecting general customer behavior or preferences, leading to suboptimal risk predictions. In this study, we introduce the Disentangled Temporal Dependencies Variational Autoencoder (DTD-VAE), an advancement over conventional VAE, designed to disentangle temporal dependencies and distinguish credit risk-related features from past customer preferences. The feature inference module of the DTD-VAE incorporates an autoregressive temporal dependency learning mechanism that adeptly captures the temporal dependencies among latent variables, enriching the model's comprehension of the inherent data structure. Furthermore, the feature generative module utilizes an element-wise gating mechanism that assigns independent weights to each dimension of the expert models, enabling a finer-grained disentanglement of latent variables, particularly those relevant to credit risk prediction. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving performance gains of 3.2%-4.86% in ROC-AUC and 6.41%-9.71% in Accuracy Ratio.

</details>


### [344] [Interpretable hybrid credit scoring for thin-file and underbanked populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26837)
*Belise Kanziga,Yaé U. Gaba,Olivier Kanamugire*

Main category: q-fin.RM

TL;DR: 本文在残差学习混合信用评分框架（逻辑回归评分卡+梯度提升修正残差，按可解释性比率ρ(x)分解）基础上沿三个维度扩展：东非实证（Zindi金融包容数据）、三分区粒度公平性审计、以及薄文件分段分析。台湾基准AUC=0.776，Zindi上AUC=0.869且Brier分数降低46%。公平审计发现农村、低教育、乌干达受访者被严重路由至不透明ML区域，而性别几乎无差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合信用评分框架虽兼具可解释性与准确性，但缺乏跨区域实证验证、缺乏在解释性分区粒度上的公平性审计，且未针对非洲金融包容场景（如薄文件群体）进行专门分析。

Method: 在原有残差学习混合框架（逻辑回归+梯度提升残差修正）基础上，在Zindi非洲金融包容数据（肯尼亚、卢旺达、坦桑尼亚、乌干达）上进行实证；对框架的三个可解释性区域分别做子群公平性审计；并开展薄文件群体分段分析。

Result: 台湾基准：AUC=0.776（较LR提升+0.057），Brier降低23%，高违约率借款人69.5%集中在完全可解释区域。Zindi：AUC=0.869（较LR提升+0.015），Brier从0.158降至0.085（降46%）。公平审计发现严重的子群路由偏差（农村vs城市18个百分点、低教育vs高教育32个百分点、乌干达vs肯尼亚22个百分点），性别几乎无差异。

Conclusion: 混合框架在东非数据上表现优异且保持可解释性集中趋势；但公平审计揭示了聚合指标掩盖的严重子群路由不公平，该审计方法可直接服务于非洲央行数字信贷监管。

Abstract: We extend a residual-learning hybrid credit scoring framework (logistic regression scorecard plus a gradient-boosting correction on its residuals, decomposed at each prediction into an interpretability ratio $ρ(x)$ that measures the share attributable to the linear branch) along three axes: an East African empirical instantiation on the Zindi Financial Inclusion in Africa data (Kenya, Rwanda, Tanzania, Uganda); a fairness audit at the granularity of the framework's three interpretability regions; and a thin-file segmentation analysis. On the Taiwan Credit Default benchmark retained for continuity, the calibrated hybrid attains AUC $= 0.776$ ($Δ\mathrm{AUC} = +0.057$ vs.\ standalone logistic regression, $+0.001$ vs.\ standalone XGBoost), reduces Brier Score by 23\%, and concentrates the highest-default-rate borrowers (69.5\%) in the fully interpretable region. On Zindi, the calibrated hybrid attains AUC $= 0.869$ ($Δ\mathrm{AUC} = +0.015$ vs.\ LR, $p < 0.001$; $-0.004$ vs.\ XGBoost), cuts Brier from $0.158$ to $0.085$ (a 46\% reduction), and replicates the regional routing pattern. The fairness audit detects severe routing into the opaque ML-driven region along socioeconomic axes: rural respondents by 18 percentage points relative to urban, primary-or-less-educated by 32 points relative to secondary-and-above, and Ugandan respondents by 22 points relative to Kenyan, while gender shows essentially no routing disparity. The audit pipeline surfaces subgroup-routing violations that aggregate fairness metrics miss, in a form directly usable by African central-bank supervisors of digital credit.

</details>


### [345] [On the approximation of posterior laws in compound loss models by conditional Wasserstein GANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27229)
*Aleksandar Arandjelovic,Pavel V. Shevchenko,George Tzougas*

Main category: q-fin.RM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件Wasserstein生成对抗网络（cWGAN）的摊销贝叶斯推断方法，用于复合损失模型中的后验近似。该方法以充分统计量、先验均值和变异系数以及先验族混合权重为条件，单个共享生成器即可同时逼近Poisson强度和高尾Pareto形状参数在Gamma、逆高斯和对数正态混合先验下的后验分布。通过基于模拟的校准与解析后验、确定性求积及MCMC进行比较验证，并在极端自然灾害损失数据上生成滚动一年期后验预测分布，分析重尾严重性和先验族不确定性对聚合尾部风险的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复合损失模型中，贝叶斯推断常常需要在不同保单、市场情景和先验设定下重复进行。在非共轭情形下，这需要重复的数值积分或MCMC采样，计算成本高昂。作者旨在构建一种摊销的后验近似方法，避免每次推断场景都从头进行昂贵计算。

Method: 将问题形式化为摊销后验近似，构建以充分统计量、先验均值、变异系数和先验族混合权重为条件的条件Wasserstein生成对抗网络（cWGAN）。单个共享生成器能够同时近似Poisson强度和Pareto形状参数在Gamma、逆高斯、对数正态先验混合下的后验分布。

Result: 通过基于模拟的校准（SBC）验证近似质量，并与解析后验、确定性求积和大量MCMC模拟进行比较。在极端自然灾害损失数据的应用中，生成滚动一年期后验预测分布，并揭示重尾严重性和先验族不确定性对聚合尾部风险的影响。

Conclusion: 所提出的单生成器cWGAN框架能够高效地摊销复合损失模型中的贝叶斯推断，不仅适用于多种先验族混合，还能准确捕捉后验分布并评估尾部风险，为精算与风险管理中的高频重复推断任务提供了计算高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Bayesian inference in compound loss models must often be repeated across policies, market scenarios, and prior specifications. Outside conjugate cases, this may require repeated numerical integration or Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC). We formulate this problem as amortized posterior approximation and construct a conditional Wasserstein generative adversarial network conditioned on sufficient statistics, prior mean and coefficient of variation, and mixture weights of prior families. Notably, a single shared generator is able to approximate the posterior laws of both the Poisson intensity and the Pareto shape parameter under mixtures of Gamma, inverse-Gaussian, and lognormal priors. We assess the approximation by simulation-based calibration and by comparisons with analytical posteriors, deterministic quadrature, and extensive MCMC simulations. In an application to data on extreme natural catastrophe losses, we produce rolling one-year posterior predictive distributions, and examine the effects of heavy-tailed severity and prior-family uncertainty on aggregate tail risk.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [346] [TRACE: Retrospective Streaming Generation of Physical Fields under Sparse Structured Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26219)
*Xinyu Zhang,Lihao Chen,Panqi Chen,Lei Cheng,Ting Zhang,Jianlong Li,Shikai Fang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一个用于结构化传感下物理场重建的回顾式流式生成重建框架。TRACE在学习的连续坐标潜空间中执行近似贝叶斯推断，将稀疏离格测量转化为生成式潜证据，通过卡尔曼式滤波与状态空间时间先验融合，并利用回顾式平滑改进欠观测历史帧。在活性物质、海洋声速场和超新星模拟上的实验表明，TRACE在时间稀疏和空间局部化的传感协议下，重建质量达到或超越帧式生成重建器、离线时空方法和流式数据同化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式重建方法大多假设固定、批量的条件设定，而真实传感系统往往产生结构化流式数据（探头扫描局部区域、仪器观测移动视野、通信约束导致整帧缺失）。因此需要一种能处理流式结构化传感的生成重建框架。

Method: TRACE在学习的连续坐标潜空间中执行近似贝叶斯推断。它将稀疏离格测量转换为生成式潜证据，与状态空间时间先验通过卡尔曼式滤波融合，并通过回顾式平滑来细化欠观测的历史帧。

Result: 在活性物质、海洋声速场和超新星模拟数据集上的实验表明，TRACE在时间稀疏和空间局部化传感协议下，重建质量达到或超越帧式生成重建器、离线时空方法和流式数据同化基线。

Conclusion: TRACE有效解决了实际传感系统中结构化流式数据的物理场重建问题，为流式生成重建提供了新范式，在多种科学应用场景中表现优异。

Abstract: Reconstructing continuous physical fields from sparse measurements is central to scientific monitoring, inverse modeling, and digital-twin construction. Generative reconstruction has recently emerged as a promising paradigm for this task by learning data-driven physical priors that complete plausible full fields from limited observations. However, existing methods largely assume fixed, batch conditioning, whereas real sensing systems often produce structured streams: probes scan local regions, instruments observe moving fields of view, and communication constraints may leave entire frames missing. We propose TRACE, a retrospective streaming generative reconstruction framework for physical fields under structured sensing. TRACE performs approximate Bayesian inference in a learned continuous-coordinate latent space, converting sparse off-grid measurements into generative latent evidence, fusing it with a state-space temporal prior through Kalman-style filtering, and refining under-observed past frames via retrospective smoothing. Experiments on active matter, ocean sound-speed fields, and supernova simulations show that TRACE matches or surpasses frame-wise generative reconstructors, offline spatiotemporal methods, and streaming data-assimilation baselines in reconstruction quality under temporally sparse and spatially localized sensing protocols.

</details>


### [347] [A Unified Descriptive-Complexity Framework for Model Selection under Correlated Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26618)
*Yanhang Zhang,Wei Liu,Yuhong Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种描述复杂度信息准则（DCIC），利用Kraft可容许的编码长度来正则化大规模候选模型集合。在次Weibull噪声下，无需RIP条件即可实现选择一致性，并给出非渐近的Oracle风险界（在模型误设定下依然有效）。该编码原理还能以较小的类别识别代价将异质模型类置于统一的复杂度尺度上，实现类别-模型恢复与跨类别风险适应。此外提出复杂度引导的搜索路径，明确计算与统计之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型选择在强预测变量依赖和模型类不确定的情况下尤为困难，特别是当候选模型数量呈指数级增长时，现有方法（如依赖RIP条件）难以同时保证选择一致性和良好的风险界，且难以在多个异质模型类之间进行公平的比较与选择。

Method: 1) 提出Descriptive-Complexity Information Criterion (DCIC)，通过Kraft-admissible编码长度为大规模候选模型正则化；2) 通过近似误差分离（approximation-error separation）在次Weibull噪声下证明选择一致性，无需RIP条件；3) 利用同一编码原理，以小的类别识别代价将异质模型类置于统一复杂度尺度；4) 开发复杂度引导的搜索路径，显式权衡计算与统计性能（大惩罚保留多项式规模的搜索区域，小惩罚则优化Oracle风险基准）。

Result: 在理论上建立了无需RIP条件的选择一致性、模型误设定下依然有效的非渐近Oracle风险界、类别-模型恢复与跨类别风险适应性质。在实践上，数值实验表明该方法在强依赖与模型类不确定情况下能实现稳定的支持恢复和良好的估计性能，且搜索路径提供了明确的计算-统计权衡。

Conclusion: DCIC为大规模模型选择提供了一种基于描述复杂度的统一框架，它不依赖RIP条件，在强预测变量依赖和模型类不确定下依然具有选择一致性与良好的风险保证，并通过复杂度引导搜索实现了计算与统计性能之间的显式平衡。

Abstract: Model selection becomes particularly challenging under strong predictor dependence and model-class uncertainty, especially when there are exponentially many models. We propose a Descriptive-Complexity Information Criterion (DCIC) that regularizes large candidate model collections through Kraft-admissible code lengths. Under sub-Weibull noise, we establish selection consistency through approximation-error separation without relying on RIP-type conditions, together with nonasymptotic oracle risk bounds that remain valid under model misspecification. The same coding principle places heterogeneous classes on a common complexity scale at a small additional class-identification cost. This extension yields class--model recovery under suitable identifiability conditions and risk adaptation across classes. We further develop a complexity-guided search path that makes the computation--statistics trade-off explicit. Large penalties yield polynomial-size retained search regions with high probability, whereas smaller penalties sharpen the oracle risk benchmark. Numerical experiments illustrate stable support recovery and favorable estimation performance under strong dependence and model-class uncertainty.

</details>


### [348] [Incremental Recommendation via Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26804)
*Athanasios Vlontzos,David Gustafsson,Michael O'Riordan,Ciarán M. Gilligan-Lee*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对推荐系统印象资源的有限性问题，将现有生产推荐模型扩展为因果架构，利用已有的holdback数据（无需新数据采集），通过双阈值定位策略解决治疗组与对照组归因窗口不匹配的问题。在Spotify数百万用户的规模A/B测试中，该策略在不显著降低推荐内容消费的前提下，减少了7%的推荐印象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐印象是有限资源，向本会自然发现内容的用户推送推荐不产生增量价值，反而挤占了其他更需要的推荐机会。现有方法忽视了这种"有机发现"与"推荐驱动"之间的区分，需要因果架构来识别真正的增量效应。

Method: 1) 将现有生产推荐模型扩展为因果架构；2) 利用常规实验基础设施中已收集的holdback数据，无需新数据采集；3) 针对治疗组（短直接响应窗口）与对照组（多日有机窗口）归因窗口不匹配问题，提出双阈值定位策略——仅在"治疗流概率高且有机流概率低"时推送推荐；4) 使用holdback数据联合训练以改善校准。

Result: 在Spotify数百万用户的生产级A/B测试中，该策略将推荐印象减少7%，且推荐内容总体消费无统计学显著下降。联合训练改善了治疗组头部的校准效果，优于生产基线。

Conclusion: 因果模型相比仅基于观测数据的模型能学习到更具泛化性的表征，且在实际生产中能高效利用有限推荐资源。双阈值策略有效解决了归因窗口不匹配问题，展示了因果推荐系统在工业界的实用价值。

Abstract: Recommendation impressions are a finite resource, hence delivering a recommendation to a user who would discover the content organically yields no incremental value and displaces other recommendations that could. We address this by extending an existing production recommendation model to a causal architecture using holdback data that is already collected as part of routine experimentation infrastructure, requiring no new data collection. A central challenge is that attribution windows differ between treated and holdback observations: treated users are attributed a stream within a short direct-response window, while holdback users are attributed organic streams over a multi-day window. This mismatch makes naive treatment-effect subtraction invalid. We resolve this with a dual-threshold targeting policy that delivers a recommendation only when the probability of a treated stream is high and the probability of organic stream is low. In a production-scale A/B test on millions of Spotify users, this policy reduces recommendation impressions by 7% with no statistically significant reduction in overall recommended content consumption. We further show that joint training with holdback data improves calibration of the treated head relative to the production baseline, and argue this can be taken as evidence that causal models learn more generalisable representations than models trained on observational data alone.

</details>


### [349] [Why not to use the Gaussian kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26974)
*Toni Karvonen,Chris J. Oates*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文论证高斯核（又称平方指数核或RBF核）应被避免作为默认核函数，因其过度光滑导致条件方差不切实际地小（引起预测过拟合/过度自信），且伴随数值病态问题。作者进一步推广论点：所有解析核函数都应避免，因解析性等价于谱密度的指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯核是高斯过程回归中最流行的核函数之一，但作者认为其作为默认选择存在严重问题：条件方差过小导致灾难性过度自信，以及数值病态需要引入nugget项等技巧（这些技巧实质改变了底层模型）。

Method: 通过两个理论结果论证：1）高斯核导致条件方差不切实际地小；2）方差小伴随数值病态。作者将问题根源归因于高斯核的解析性（analyticity），并论证对平稳核而言，解析性基本等价于谱密度的指数衰减，从而推广到所有解析核都应避免。

Result: 证明高斯核在预测不确定性量化和数值稳定性两方面均具有极端脆弱性，且问题本质源于核的解析性而非高斯形式本身。

Conclusion: 高斯核不应作为默认核函数，更广泛地，解析核都应避免使用；建议采用非解析的核函数（如Matern类核）以保证数值稳定性和合理的预测不确定性。

Abstract: Kernels measure similarity or correlation in tasks such as regression and classification. The Gaussian kernel, other names of which include squared exponential and radial basis function kernel, is one of the most popular in Gaussian process regression. We argue that the Gaussian kernel is best avoided and should never be used as a default. The argument rests on two results demonstrating that the Gaussian kernel is extremely brittle. First, the Gaussian kernel gives rise to a conditional variance that is unrealistically small. If the variance is used to quantify predictive uncertainty, catastrophic overconfidence is almost inevitable. Second, a small variance goes hand in hand with numerical ill-conditioning, so that to use the Gaussian kernel in practice requires tricks such as nugget terms that effectively modify the underlying regression or classification model. These problems are caused by the unnatural smoothness of the Gaussian kernel, a fact we are far from the first to take notice of. The problem is not the Gaussian form itself but the analyticity of the kernel: Our argument is more broadly that analytic kernels are best avoided. For stationary kernels analyticity is essentially equivalent to an exponential decay of the spectral density.

</details>


### [350] [Representation Measurements Under Function-Preserving Reparameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27020)
*Abdullah Karasan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了列置换平行分析（column-permutation parallel analysis）违反函数保持的基变换不变性（function-preserving reparameterization invariance），其参考分布和选定的成分数量会随隐藏坐标的选择而变化，即使模型函数和观测协方差谱保持不变。这意味着平行分析得到的成分计数和决策可能反映隐藏坐标的人为选择，而非模型自身的固有属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的隐藏坐标并非由其输入输出函数唯一确定，因此基于表示（representation）的测量应当对保持函数不变的基变换具有不变性。然而，平行分析（parallel analysis）作为一种常用的表示维度选择方法，其是否满足这一不变性尚未得到严格检验，这正是本研究的出发点。

Method: 研究首先从理论上证明列置换平行分析违反函数保持的再参数化不变性，并进一步论证数据内部参考程序无法同时满足保持每个坐标边际、保持正交等变性以及消除跨坐标协方差这三个性质。在实证上，研究跨越五个模型、三个检索领域和75种变换进行测试，并设计了仅居中的对照实验（centering-only control）来隔离参考分布驱动的效应，同时与正交不变比较器分数（orthogonally invariant comparator scores）进行对比。

Result: 实证结果显示，中位成分计数分歧为0.79，中位固定阈值决策分歧为0.26。在仅居中的对照实验中，1200个成分计数中有1141个发生改变，而观测谱并未变化；相比之下，独立的平行分析种子不改变任何对应决策。正交不变比较器分数则保持数值稳定，且具有相似的保留判别能力。

Conclusion: 平行分析衍生的成分计数和决策可能反映隐藏坐标的选择而非模型良定义的性质。这一发现对基于表示的模型分析方法的有效性和可解释性提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Hidden coordinates are not uniquely determined by a language model's input--output function, so representation-derived measurements should be invariant to function-preserving changes of basis. This study shows that column-permutation parallel analysis violates function-preserving reparameterization invariance because its reference distribution and selected component count can change while the model function and observed covariance spectrum remain fixed. More generally, a data-internal reference procedure cannot simultaneously preserve every coordinate marginal, remain orthogonally equivariant, and remove cross-coordinate covariance. Empirically, across five models, three retrieval domains, and 75 transformations, median component-count disagreement is 0.79 and median fixed-threshold decision disagreement is 0.26. A centering-only control isolates the reference-driven effect, with 1,141 of 1,200 component counts changing despite an unchanged observed spectrum, whereas independent parallel analysis seeds change none of the corresponding decisions. By contrast, orthogonally invariant comparator scores remain numerically stable with similar held-out discrimination. Together, these results show that parallel analysis-derived component counts and decisions can reflect hidden-coordinate choice rather than a well-defined property of the model.

</details>


### [351] [Active Diffusion-Based Inference for Ill-Posed Inverse Problems under Incomplete Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27080)
*Jitao Xu,Nobuo Sato,Yaohang Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于主动扩散模型（diffusion model）的反问题求解器。该方法训练DM学习参数空间到可观测空间的映射，通过迭代检测和校正模型误设定（基于后验不确定性），即使初始训练边界不包含真实参数，也能发现并学习正确的参数区域。作者在具有无限解的反问题玩具案例以及量子色动力学（QCD）核子结构分析中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反问题（从可观测数据估计未知参数）在科学和工程中普遍存在，但面临非线性、噪声和不适定性（ill-posedness）等固有挑战。此外，传统方法在初始训练域不包含真实参数时（即先验知识不完整）会失效，因此需要一种能够自适应扩展搜索域并保持贝叶斯推断原则性的求解方法。

Method: 1) 训练一个扩散模型（DM）来学习参数空间到可观测空间之间的映射。2) 通过迭代检测模型误设定（model misspecification），利用后验不确定性来识别当前模型覆盖不足的区域。3) 通过自适应域扩展（adaptive domain augmentation），逐步发现并学习包含真实参数的正确参数区域。该方法为自适应域扩展提供了贝叶斯理论依据。

Result: 方法在两个场景中得到验证：(1) 一个具有无限解的反问题玩具案例；(2) 在量子色动力学（QCD）核子结构分析中，将量子关联函数参数化到事件可观测量。结果表明该方法在初始训练边界排除真实参数的情况下仍能实现稳健的推断。

Conclusion: 该工作为不完全先验知识下的反问题推断提供了一种有原则的、贝叶斯合理化的求解框架，通过主动学习与扩散模型的结合，实现了对正确参数区域的自适应发现，确保了反问题推断的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Many scientific and engineering applications require estimating unknown parameters from experimentally observable data -- an inverse problem that is inherently challenging due to nonlinearity, noise, and ill-posedness. In this paper, we propose an active diffusion-based inverse problem solver. A DM is trained to learn the mapping between the parameter space and the observable space. By iteratively detecting and correcting model misspecification through posterior uncertainty, the method discovers and learns the correct region of parameter space, even when initial training bounds exclude the true parameters. This provides a principled, Bayesian justification for adaptive domain augmentation and ensures robust inference for inverse problems under incomplete prior knowledge. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our inverse solver for a toy inverse problem with infinite solutions, and for the parameterization of the quantum correlation functions to event observables in a Quantum Chromodynamics analysis of nucleon structure.

</details>


### [352] [Recovering Expert Critic-Sourced Network Adjacency between Musical Artists from Acoustic Distributions: A Construct-Validity Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27291)
*Elena Badillo-Goicoechea,Fengfeng He*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一个第三类音乐推荐信号：评论家对艺术家的"关键邻接"关系（critic adjacency）。通过将艺术家表示为80维Essentia声学描述符的经验分布，并用Wasserstein最优输运距离建模配对相似度，在冷启动分裂下以0.767的AUC恢复了评论家建立的艺术家人际边，表明批评话语中存在着可复现的"声学核心"与"社会学剩余"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐推荐主要依赖两类信号：用户-物品交互（冷启动失效）和音乐内容本身。作者认为存在第三类未被充分挖掘且更 principled 的信号——评论家在长篇散文中明确建立的两两艺术家关系（关键邻接）。先前工作已验证其内部效度（能恢复连贯社区、可匹配协同过滤），但缺乏外部效度验证（即这种关系是否真正植根于音乐本身而非社会语境），本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将艺术家表示为80个Essentia低层声学描述符上的经验分布，用边际最优输运（Wasserstein）距离建模两两邻近度，在冷启动、艺术家不相交的划分下评估关键邻接边在声学上的可恢复性。采用集成模型，并按关键共识度分层、按体裁分层进行评估。

Result: 集成模型在样本外AUC达到0.767（95% CI 0.761-0.775）。可恢复性随关键共识单调上升，多源认证边达到0.865。分层评估符合社会学体裁模型：紧密界定的场景型体裁可恢复性高于宽泛行业伞式术语。

Conclusion: 评论话语是推荐的丰富信息来源，可分解为可复现的"声学核心"和由叙事定位、亚文化语境、经典安置驱动的"社会学剩余"。工作提供了可扩展的冷启动发现机制，并为MIR和MRS研究提供了社会学视角的方法。

Abstract: Music recommendation relies primarily on two signals: user-item interactions, which fail in the cold-start regime, and intrinsic musical content, available for any recording. We argue that a third, largely untapped signal is both richer and more principled: critical adjacency, the pairwise relation established when an expert critic explicitly links two artists in long-form prose. It encodes deliberate judgments about which artists belong together. Prior work established its internal validity, showing it recovers coherent, interpretable communities and can match collaborative filtering in user-satisfaction simulations, with no user data. What has been missing is external validation: whether this critic-sourced relation is grounded in the music itself versus sociological context. We test it against acoustic content, reframing the question as one of construct validity.
  Representing artists as empirical distributions over 80 low-level Essentia acoustic descriptors and modeling pairwise proximity via marginal optimal-transport (Wasserstein) distances, we evaluate how far critical adjacency is sonically recoverable under a cold-start, artist-disjoint split. Our ensemble recovers these edges at out-of-sample AUC of 0.767 (95% CI 0.761-0.775). Recoverability rises monotonically with critical consensus, reaching 0.865 on multi-source attested edges. Stratified evaluations align with sociological models of genre: tightly bounded, scene-based genres show higher recoverability than broad industry umbrella terms.
  Critical discourse is thus a rich source of information for recommendation, decomposing into a reproducible "sonic core" and a "sociological remainder" driven by narrative positioning, subcultural context, and canonical placement. The work offers both a scalable cold-start discovery mechanism and a sociologically grounded approach to MIR and MRS research.

</details>


### [353] [A Finite Sample Analysis for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27313)
*Zijie Cheng,Xiang Li,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为表格型分布强化学习中的同步分位数时序差分学习（QTD）建立了全局有限样本保证。证明通过全局比较论证和局部线性化两个机制分离，最终得到last-iterate波动为Õ(T^{-a/2}/√(1-γ))，且与分位数数量无多项式依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习中的QTD算法缺乏全局有限样本保证，已有分析通常局限于局部收敛或依赖较强的假设条件。作者希望建立从任意初始化出发的全局收敛保证，并明确区分随机波动与样本复杂度的不同来源。

Method: 证明分两步进行：(1) 全局比较论证：利用奖励累积分布函数的序单调性和分布Bellman算子的W∞压缩性，将任意初始化的迭代引入局部邻域；(2) 局部线性化分析：在邻域内对QTD平均场进行线性化，其Jacobian是非奇异M-矩阵，利用相关的正半群进行方差敏感的鞅分析。

Result: 对于步长α_t=c(t+1)^{-a}（a∈(1/2,1)），leading last-iterate波动为Õ(T^{-a/2}/√(1-γ))，且不依赖于分位数数量（无多项式依赖）。确定性暂态和burn-in时间可能依赖于最小的Bellman目标密度，最坏情况下为m^{-1}阶。

Conclusion: 结果清晰地区分了局部随机波动（与分位数无关、与1-γ呈平方根倒数关系）与全局样本复杂度（可能受最小Bellman目标密度m^{-1}限制）之间的差异，为分布RL的QTD算法提供了完整的理论刻画。

Abstract: We establish a global finite-sample guarantee for synchronous quantile temporal-difference learning (QTD) in tabular distributional reinforcement learning. The proof separates two stability mechanisms. A global comparison argument, based on the order monotonicity of reward cumulative distribution functions and the $W_\infty$ contraction of the distributional Bellman operator, brings an arbitrarily initialized iterate into a local neighborhood. Inside that neighborhood, we linearize the QTD mean field. Its Jacobian is a nonsingular $M$-matrix, and the associated positive semigroup permits a variance-sensitive martingale analysis. For stepsizes $α_t=c(t+1)^{-a}$ with $a\in(1/2,1)$, the leading last-iterate fluctuation is of order $\widetilde O\bigl(T^{-a/2}/\sqrt{1-γ}\bigr)$ and has no polynomial dependence on the number of quantiles. The deterministic transient and the required burn-in can still depend on the smallest Bellman-target density, which is of order $m^{-1}$ in the worst case. The result therefore distinguishes sharply between the local stochastic fluctuation and the global sample complexity.

</details>
